"cīta muṟṟōṉ avinayam ceppiṉ, ōtiya paruvara luḷḷamō ṭuḻattalu, mīra mākiya pōrvi yuṟuttalu, māra veyiluḻan taḻalum vēṇṭalu, muraciyu muraṉṟu muyirttu murittalun, takkaṉa piṟavuñ cāṟṟiṉar pulavar" ;
"veppiṉ avinayam virikkuṅ kālit, tappil kaṭip piṭit taṉmiyun tākamum, eriyiṉaṉṉa vemmiyō ṭiyivum, veruvaru miyakkamum vempiya viḻiyum, nīruṇ vēṭkiyu nirampā valiyum, ōruṅ kāli uṇarntaṉar koḷalē" ; 
"koñciya moḻiyiṟ vāyiṟ paṉinuri kūmpalun, tañca māntar tammuka nōkkiyōr, iṉco liyampuvāṉ pōliyam pāmiyum, nañcuṇ ṭōṉṟaṉ avinayam eṉpa" ;
"colliya vaṉṟiyum varuvaṉa vuḷaveṉiṟ, pulluvaḻic cērttip poruntuvaḻip puṇarppa."
iṉi, col eṉṉum uṟuppu mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avi; uṭcol, puṟaccol, ākāyac col eṉpaṉa. neñcoṭu kūṟal uṭcollum, kēṭpōrk kurittal puṟaccollum, tāṉē kūṟal ākāyaccollum ām.
colvaki  nāṉku vakippaṭum. avi; cuṇṇam, curitakam, vaṇṇam, varitakam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, cuṇṇam nāṉkaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; curitakam eṭṭaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; vaṇṇam patiṉāṟaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; varitakam muppattiraṇṭaṭiyāṉ varuvatu.
vaṇṇamāṉatu oru vakiyāṉ peruvaṇṇam, iṭi vaṇṇam, vaṉappu vaṇṇameṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭumeṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ, peruvaṇṇam āṟāyum, iṭivaṇṇam irupat toṉṟāyum, vaṉappuvaṇṇam nāṟpattoṉṟāyum varumeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār1  irupatu vaṇṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. nūṟuvaṇṇaṅ kūṟiṉārumuḷar.
variyāvatu; varikkūttukkuriya pāṭal.
"varippāṭalāvatu; paṇṇum, tiṟamum, ceyalum, pāṇiyum oru neṟiyaṉṟi mayaṅkac collappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉiyalpum, āṟaṉiyalpum peṟṟut, taṉ mutalum iṟutiyuṅ keṭṭu, iyalpum muṭamumāka muṭintu, karutappaṭṭa cantiyum cārttum peṟṟum peṟātum varum" eṉṟum, "atutāṉ, teyvañ cuṭṭiyum makkaḷip paḻicciyum varum" eṉṟum kūṟuvar arumpata uriyāciriyar, varippāṭṭukkaḷ tiṇinili vari, kiṇinili vari eṉavum, mukamuṭi vari, mukamil vari, paṭippu vari eṉavum, avi palavākavum pāku pāṭeytum. avaṟṟiṉiyalpellām, piṉṉar, kāṉalvari yuḷḷum, vēṉiṟkātiyuḷḷum viḷaṅkaluṟum.
iṉi, canti yeṉpatu nāṭṭiyak kaṭṭuriyiṉ pirivukaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarntu niṟkum nili. atu, mukam, piratimukam, karuppam, viḷivu, tuyttal eṉa aivakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ mukamā vatu eḻuvakippaṭṭa uḻaviṉāṟ camikkappaṭṭa pūmiyuḷ iṭṭa vittup paruvañ ceytu muḷittu muṭivatu pōlvatu; pirati mukamāvatu aṅṅaṉam muḷittal mutalāy ilitōṉṟi nāṟṟāy muṭivatu pōlvatu; karuppamāvatu an nāṟṟu mutalāyk karuviruntu perukit taṉṉuṭ poruḷ potintu karuppamuṟṟi niṟpatu pōlvatu; viḷivāvatu karuppa mutalāy virintu katir tiraṇṭiṭṭuk kāy tāḻntu muṟṟi viḷintu muṭivatu pōlvatu; tuyttalāvatu viḷiyappaṭṭa poruḷi aṟuttup pōriṭṭuk kaṭāviṭṭut tūṟṟip poli ceytu koṇṭupōy uṇṭu makiḻvatu pōlvatu. vittu, nāṟṟu, karu, viḷivu, tuyttal eṉpaṉa oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarntu muṭitalpōla nāṭakattiṟ kuriya kati aintu pakutiyāy oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarntu muṭiya vēṇṭum eṉṟapaṭi. ivvuṟuppu nāṭakattiṟ kiṉṟiyamiyāta toṉṟeṉka.
cētam eṉpatu ātik katiyi āriyam, tamiḻ eṉṉum iruvakik kūttiṟkēṟpac cētittiṭuvatu eṉpar;
   ‘	āriyat tamiḻeṉuñ cīrnaṭa miraṇṭiṉum
ātik katiyi yavaṟṟiṟ koppac
cētit tiṭuvatu cētameṉ ṟākum.'
eṉpatu kāṇka. 'vilakkiṉiṟ puṇarttu' eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭa vilakkuṟuppukkaḷ itukāṟum uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa 
14. 		patiṉōrāṭalāvaṉa; acurarik kolla amararāṭiya pati ṉoru kūttukkaḷ. avaṟṟi.       
  "kaṭiyamayi rāṇimarak kālvinti kantaṉ
kuṭituṭimā lalliyamaṟ kumpam - cuṭarviḻiyāṟ
paṭṭamataṉ pēṭutirup pāviyaraṉ pāṇṭaraṅkam
koṭṭiyivi kāṇpatiṉōr kūttu"
eṉpataṉālaṟika. ivi niṉṟāṭal, paṭintāṭal eṉa iruvakiyiṉa;
  "alliyaṅ koṭṭi kuṭikuṭam pāṇṭaraṅkam
malluṭa ṉiṉṟāṭa lāṟu"
  "	tuṭikaṭiyam pēṭu marakkālē pāvi
vaṭivuṭaṉ vīḻntāṭa lintu"
ikkūttu ovvoṉṟum evvek kāraṇampaṟṟi evvev viṭattu nikaḻttappaṭṭaṉaveṉpatu piṉṉark 1kaṭalāṭu kātiyāl viḷakkamām.
14. 		pāṭṭu eṉṟatu aka nāṭakaṅkaḷukkum, puṟanāṭakaṅ kaḷukku muriya urukkaḷi. aka nāṭakaṅkaḷukkuriya uruvāvaṉa; kanta mutal pirapantavuru vīṟāka irupatteṭṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kanta meṉpatu aṭivariyaṟi yuṭittāy oru tāḷattāṟ puṇarppatu; pirapanta meṉpatu aṭivariyaṟi yiṉṟip pala tāḷat tāṟ puṇarppatu. puṟanāṭakaṅkaḷukkuriya uruvāvaṉa; tēva pāṇi mutalāka araṅkoḻi ceyyu ḷīṟākac centuṟi vikaṟ paṅka ḷellāmeṉka.
14. 		koṭṭu - koṭṭappaṭum vācciyaṅkaḷ. avi kītāṅ kam, niruttāṅkam, upayāṅkam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, kītāṅkam kītattiṟku vācippatu; niruttāṅkam niruttattiṟku vācippatu; upayāṅkam iraṇṭiṟkum vācippatu. 
15. 	koḷki - nūl, āṅku, aci.. 
16. 		āṭal - muṟkūṟiya akakkūttiṉum puṟakkūttiṉumuḷḷa āṭal. kīṟṟu, kaṭicari mutalākiya tēcikkuriya kālkaḷ iru pattu nāṉkum, cuṟṟutal, eṟital mutalākiya vaṭukiṟkuriya kālkaḷ patiṉāṉkum, uṭaṟṟūkku mutalākiya uṭalavart taṉi oṉpatum akakkūttuk kuriyaṉa. "ciṅkaḷam iruvaki niliyiṉum eytum" eṉpārum, uṭalavart taṉiyic ciṅkaḷa meṉpārumuḷar. eṉavē, tamiḻ, vaṭuku, ciṅkaḷam eṉa āṭal mūvakippaṭum eṉka.
pāṭal - iṉpam, teḷivu, niṟi, oḷi, vaṉcol, iṟuti, mantam, uccam eṉṉum eṇvakip payaṉumuṭiya pāṭal .
pāṇi - tāḷam ; atu koṭṭu, aci, tūkku, aḷavu eṉa nāṉku niliyiṉiyuṭiyatu ; koṭṭāvatu amukkutal; aciyāvatu tākki yeḻutal ; tūkkāvatu tākkit tūkkutal ; aḷavāvatu tākkiṉa vōci nērē mūṉṟu māttiri peṟumaḷavum varutal. koṭṭu ari māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, ka ; aci oru māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, e ; tūkku iraṇṭu māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, u ; aḷavu mūṉṟu māttiri ; ataṟku vaṭivu, ḵ eṉak koḷka. koṭṭu mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ vaṭivik kuṟikka, 'ka' mutaliya iṭukuṟiyāka niṟuttap peṟṟaṉa veṉka;
"	koṭṭum aciyun tūkkum aḷavum
oṭṭap puṇarppatu pāṇi yākum"
"	kakaraṅ koṭṭē ekaram aciyē
ukaran tūkkē aḷavē yāytam"
eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
 ari māttiriyuṭiya ēkatāḷam mutalākap patiṉāṟu māt tiriyuṭiya pārvati lōcaṉam īṟākac coṉṉa nāṟpattoru tāḷam puṟakkūttukkuriya veṉavum, āṟaṉ maṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, eṭṭaṉ maṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, tāḷavoriyal eṉpaṉavum,  taṉinili yoriyal eṉpaṉavum, oṉṟaṉ pāṇi mutalāka eṇkūttup pāṇi yīṟākak kiṭanta patiṉoru pāṇi vikaṟpaṅ kaḷum, mutalnaṭi, vāram mutalāyiṉavum akakkūttiṟkuriya veṉavum kūṟuvar.
16.		tūkku - ittāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻivarum eḻuvakippaṭṭa tūkkukkaḷ ; avi centūkku, matalit tūkku, tuṇiput tūkku, kōyiṟṟūkku, nivapput tūkku, kaḻāṟṟūkku, neṭun tūkku eṉpaṉa ; eṉṉi? "orucīr centūk kirucīr matali, muccīr tuṇipu nāṟcīr kōyil, aiñcīr nivappē yaṟucīr kaḻālē, eḻucīr neṭuntūk keṉmaṉār pulavar" eṉṟā rākaliṉ.
 17. 		koḷuttutal - āṭṭuvittal. 
18. 		piṇṭi - oṉṟu ; īṇṭu oṟṟik kikku ākupeyar. piṇiyal - iṇital ; īṇṭu iraṭṭikki. piṇṭi, oṟṟikki, iṇiyā viṉikki eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa; piṇiyal, iṇikki, iraṭṭikki eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa. avinayakki iṅṅaṉam iruvakippaṭum.  piṇṭi - poruṭki yām ; piṇiyal - toḻiṟkiyām eṉka. avaṟṟuḷ oṟ ṟikki muppattu mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. avi: patāki-1, tiripatāki-2, kattariki-3, tūpam-4, arāḷam-5, iḷampiṟi-6, cukatuṇṭam-7, muṭṭi-8, kaṭakam-9, cūci-10, kamala kōcikam-11, kāṅkūlam-12, kapittam- 13, viṟpaṭi-14, kuṭaṅki-15, alāpattiram-16, pira maram-17, tāmpira cūṭam-18, pacācam-19, mukuḷam-20, piṇṭi-21, terinili-22, meynnili-23, uṉṉam- 24, maṇṭalam-25, caturam-26, māṉṟali-27, caṅku -28, vaṇṭu-29, ilati-30, kapōtam-31, makara mukam-32, valampuri-33 eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ,
patākiyāvatu nāṉku viralum tammuḷ oṭṭi nimirap peru viral kuñcittu niṟpatu.
tiripatākiyāvatu patākikkiyiṉ aṇiviral muṭakkuvatu.
kattarikiyāvatu tiripatākiyiṉ muṭaṅkiya aṇiviraṟ puṟatta tākiya naṭuviralic cuṭṭu viralōṭu porunta nimirppatu.
tūpamāvatu nimirnta naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum pātippaṭa vaḷiya niṟpatu.
arāḷamāvatu peruviral kuñcittuc cuṭṭuviraṉ muṭakki oḻinta viraṉ mūṉṟum nimirntu vaḷivatu.
iḷampiṟiyāvatu cuṭṭuviralum naṭuviralum aṇiviraliyum ciṟuviraliyum oṭṭi akam vaḷiya, vaḷitta peruviral avaṟṟi viṭṭu nīṅkuvatu ; "cuṭṭum pēṭum anāmiki ciṟuviral, oṭṭi yakam vaḷiya vocitta peruviral, viṭṭu nīṅkum vitiyiṟ ṟeṉpa."
cukatuṇṭam: "cukatuṇṭa meṉpatu toḻilpeṟak kiḷap piṉ, cuṭṭu viralum peruviral tāṉum, oṭṭi yukirnuṉi kavvi muṉvaḷin, tanāmiki muṭaṅkap pēṭṭoṭu ciṟuviral, tāṉmika nimirnta takutit teṉpa."
anāmiki - aṇiviral; pavuttiraviral. pēṭu - naṭuviral.
muṭṭi : "muṭṭi yeṉpatu moḻiyuṅ kālic, cuṭṭu naṭuviral anāmiki ciṟuviral, iṟuka muṭakki yivaṟṟiṉmicip peruviral, muṟukap piṭitta muṟimit teṉpa."
kaṭakam : "kaṭaka mukamē karutuṅ kālip, peruvira ṉuṉiyuñ cuṭṭuvira ṉuṉiyu, maruva vaḷintav vukir nuṉi kuvi, yoḻinta mūṉṟum vaḻivaḻi nimira, moḻintaṉa reṉpa muṭipaṟin tōrē."
cūci : "cūci yeṉpatu tuṇiyuṅ kāli, naṭuviral peruvira leṉṟivi tammi, laṭivuṭa ṉoṟṟic cuṭṭuvira ṉimira, voḻintaṉa vaḻivaḻi muṭaṅki niṟpa, moḻintaṉar mātō muṭipaṟintōrē."
patumakōcikam : "patuma kōcikam pakaruṅ kāli, yoppak kivaḷit tintu viralu, meyppaṭa vakaṉṟa vitiyiṟ ṟākum."
kāṅkūlamāvatu : "kāṅkū lammē karutuṅ kālic, cuṭṭum pēṭum peruviraṉ mūṉṟu, moṭṭimuṉ kuviya vanāmiki muṭaṅkic, ciṟuvira ṉimirnta ceykit tākum," "mukiḻkāṅ kūla muntuṟa moḻinta, kuvikāṅ kūlaṅ kuviviḻan tatuvē," "malarkāṅ kūla matumalarn tatuvē."
kapittamāvatu : cuṭṭuviraṉuṉiyum peruviraṉuṉiyum ukir nuṉi kuvippiṭitta oḻinta mūṉṟuviralum melleṉap piṭippatu.
viṟpiṭi : "viṟpiṭi yeṉpatu virikkuṅ kālic, cuṭṭoṭu pēṭi yanāmiki ciṟuvira, loṭṭi yakappāl vaḷiyap peruviral, viṭṭu nimirum vitiyiṟ ṟākum."
kuṭaṅkiyāvatu ellā viraluṅ kūṭṭi uṭkuḻippatu.
alāpattiramāvatu ciṟuviraṉ mutalākiya vintum vaḷintu maṟivatu.
piramaramāvatu anāmiki viralum naṭuviralum tammiṟ porunti valañcāyap peruviral naṭuvirali ṉuḷḷē cērac cuṭṭu viralum ciṟuviralum piṉpē vaḷintu niṟpatu.
tāmpiracūṭamāvatu naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum peruviralum tammil nuṉiyottuk kūṭi vaḷintu ciṟuviralum aṇiviralum muṭaṅki nimirvatu.
pacācamāvatu peruviralum cuṭṭuviralumaṉṟi oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum tammiṟ polintu niṟpateṉakkoḷka.
ap pacācantāṉ mūṉṟu vakippaṭum : akanilippacācam-cuṭṭuvira ṉuṉiyil peruvira lakappaṭuvatu.
mukanilippacācam-avviraṉ mukaṅkūṭi ukirviṭṭu niṟpatu.
ukirnilippacācam-cuṭṭuviralum peruviralum ukirnuṉi kuvi niṟpatu.
mukuḷamāvatu aintuviralun tammil talikuvintu uyarntu niṟpatu.
piṇṭiyeṉṟatu cuṭṭuviral pēṭiviral anāmiki ciṟuviral oṭṭi nekiḻa muṭaṅka avaṟṟiṉmēlē kuṟukkiṭam peruviral kaṭṭi vilaṅki viralvaḻi muṟiyoṟṟal.
teriniliyāvatu ellāviralum virintu kuñcittu niṟpatu.
meynniliyāvatu ciṟuviralum aṇiviralum naṭu viralum cuṭṭu viralum viṭṭunimirac cuṭṭuviraṉmēṟ peruviral cēravippatu.
uṉṉamāvatu ciṟuviralum peruviralum tammuṭkūṭa oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum viṭṭunimirvatu.
maṇṭalam : "maṇṭalameṉpatu mācaṟak kiḷappiṟ, pēṭu nuṉiyum peruvira ṉuṉiyuṅ, kūṭi vaḷintutam mukirnuṉi kuvi, yoḻinta mūṉṟu mokka vaḷivateṉa, moḻintaṉa reṉpa muḻu tuṇarn tōrē."
caturamāvatu cuṭṭu viralum naṭuviralum aṇiviralum tammuṭ cērntu iṟiñcap peruviral akam vara vittuc ciṟuviral piṉpē nimirntu niṟpatu.
māṉṟaliyāvatu peruviraluñ ciṟuviralu moḻinta mūṉṟun tammil ottu oṉṟi muṉṉē iṟiñci niṟpatu.
caṅkamāvatu peruviral nimira oḻinta nāṉkuviralum vaḷintu niṟpatu.
vaṇṭāvatu peruviralum aṇiviralum vaḷintu nuṉi yoṉṟic ciṟuviral nimirntu cuṭṭu viralum naṭuviralum nekiḻa vaḷintu niṟpatu.
ilatiyāvatu naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum kūṭinimirap peruviral avaṟṟiṉ kīḻvari cēra oḻinta iraṇṭu viralum vaḻimuṟi piṉṉē nimirntu niṟpatu.
kapōtameṉṟatu patākik kiyiṟ peruviral viṭṭu nimirvatu.
makaramukamāvatu peruviralum cuṭṭuviralum nimirntukūṭa oḻinta mūṉṟuviralun tammuḷoṉṟi ataṟku vēṟāy niṟpatu.
valampuriyāvatu ciṟuviralum peruviralu nimirntu cuṭṭu viraliṉ akam vaḷintu oḻinta viraṇṭum nimirntu iṟiñci niṟpatu.
iṉi, iraṭṭikki patiṉintu vakippaṭum. avi : añcali -1, puṭpāñcali-2, patumāñcali-3, kapōtam-4, kaṟ kaṭakam-5, cuvattikam-6, kaṭakāvaruttam-7, niṭatam-8, tōram-9, uṟcaṅkam-10, puṭpapuṭam-11, makaram-12, cayantam-13, apayavattam-14, varuttamāṉam-15. ivaṟṟuḷ, 
añcaliyāvatu : iraṇṭu kiyum patākiyāy akamoṉṟuvatu. 
puṭpāñcaliyāvatu iraṇṭu kiyum kuṭaṅkiyāy vantu oṉṟuvatu.
kapōtameṉṟatu iraṇṭu kiyum kapōtamākak kūṭṭuvatu.
kaṟkaṭaka meṉṟatu terinilik kiyiraṇṭum aṅkuli piṇintu varuvatu.
kavattika meṉṟatu maṇikkaṭṭiṟ poruntiya patākiyiraṇṭiyum maṇikkaṭṭi lēṟṟi vippatu.
kaṭakāvaruttamāvatu iraṇṭu kiyum kaṭakamāy maṇik kaṭṭukku ēṟa iyintu niṟpatu.
niṭatamāvatu muṭṭiyāka iraṇṭu kiyum camañceyvatu.
tōramāvatu iraṇṭu kiyum patākiyākki akampuṟa moṉṟi muṉtāḻntu niṟpatu.
uṟcaṅkamāvatu oruki piṟikkiyākak koṇṭu  oruki arāḷamākki iraṇṭu kiyum maṇikkaṭṭi lēṟṟi vippatu.
puṭpapuṭamāvatu kuṭaṅkiyiraṇṭum tammiṟ pakkaṅ kāṭṭi niṟpatu.
makarameṉṟatu kapōta miraṇṭu kiyum akampuṟa moṉṟa vippatu.
cayantam : (viṭupaṭṭatu)
apayavattamāvatu irukiyum cukatuṇṭamāka neñcuṟa nōkki nekiḻntu niṟpatu.
varuttamāṉamāvatu mukuḷak kiyiṟ kapōtak kiyi etiriṭṭuc cērppatu.
18. 		eḻiṟki - aḻakupeṟak kāṭṭuṅki. toḻiṟki - toḻil peṟak kāṭṭuṅ ki. poruṭki yeṉpatum varuvikkappaṭum ; āvatu, poruḷuṟak kāṭṭuṅki.
19. 		koṇṭavaki yaṟitalāvatu - piṇṭiyum piṇiyalum puṟakkūttukkuriya ki yeṉṟum, eḻiṟkiyum toḻiṟkiyum akakkūttukkuriya ki yeṉṟum aṟital.
20 - 21 	kūṭi, vāram eṉpaṉa cila tāḷavikaṟpaṅkaḷukkum, icip pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷukkum, avinayak ki kaṭkum, piṟa cilavaṟṟiṟkum peyar ; īṇṭu avinayak kikaḷi yuṇartti niṉṟaṉa.
kūṭi - oṟṟikki ; iraṭṭi yoṟṟik kiyākiya kuvitta kiyiyum itu kuṟikkum. vāram - iraṭṭikki. akakkūttu nikaḻumiṭattu oṟṟiyiṟ ceyta kittoḻil iraṭṭiyiṟ pukāmalum, iraṭṭiyiṟ ceyta kittoḻil oṟṟiyiṟ pukāmalum kaḷital. iṉṉum, tēciyiṟ kittoḻil mārkkattup pukāmalum, mārkkattuk kittoḻil tēciyiṟ pukāmalum kaḷitaleṉṟumām ; oṟṟiyum, iraṭṭiyum tēcikkūṟākalāṉum iraṭṭiyum iraṭṭik kiraṭṭiyum vaṭukiṟ kūṟākalāṉumeṉka. 
22 - 23 	piṇṭi - poruṭki. āṭal - piṇiyal ; toḻiṟki. puṟakkūttil āṭal nikaḻumiṭattu avinayam nikaḻāmalum, avinayam nikaḻumiṭattu āṭal nikaḻāmalum kaḷital. 
24. 		kuravi - kuravikkūttu ; atu, kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷākak kuravic ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉum kipiṇittāṭuvatu ; ataṉ iyalpiṉi, "kuravi yeṉpatu kūṟuṅ kālic, ceytōr ceyta kāmamum viṟalum, eyta vurikku miyalpiṟ ṟeṉpa" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika.
vari - varikkūttu ; aḵtu avaravar piṟanta nilattaṉmiyum piṟappiṟkēṟṟa toḻiṟ ṟaṉmiyum tōṉṟa naṭittal. itaṟku varicceyyuḷ pāṭṭākum. itaṉ iyalpiṉi, "variyeṉap paṭuvatu vakukkuṅ kālip, piṟanta nilaṉuñ ciṟanta toḻilum, aṟiyak kūṟi yāṟṟuḻi vaḻaṅkal" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. varikkūttu oruvar perumpālum vēṟṟurut tāṅki naṭippatu.
it toṭarnilic ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ, mulli cārntu āycciyar kuraviyum, kuṟiñci cārntu kuṉṟak kuraviyum, neytal cārntu kāṉalvariyum, pāli cārntu vēṭṭuva variyum, marutañ cārntu ūr cūḻvariyum nikaḻntaṉavāka amintiruttal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
varikkūttāṉatu kaṇkūṭuvari, kāṉalvari, uḷvari, puṟavari, kiḷarvari, tērccivari, kāṭcivari, eṭuttukkōḷvari eṉa eṇ vakippaṭum. ivaṟṟiṉiyalpellām vēṉiṟkātiyāl viḷakkamām. iṉi, "iv vari yeṉpataṉip palvarik kūtteṉpāru muḷar" eṉṟu kūṟi, aṭiyārkku nallār ataṟkeṭuttuk kāṭṭiya kaliveṇ pāṭṭoṉṟāl tamiḻakattē paṇṭināḷil vaḻaṅkiya ettaṉiyō vakiyāṉa kūttukkaḷum pāṭṭukkaḷum pulaṉā kiṉṟaṉa; acceyyuḷ piṉvaruvatu :
"	cintup piḻukki yuṭaṉcanti yōrmuli
konti kavuci kuṭappiḻukki-kantaṉpāṭ
ṭālaṅkāṭ ṭāṇṭi parumaṇa ṉellicci
cūlan tarunaṭṭan tūṇṭiluṭaṉ-cīlamikum
āṇṭi yamaṇpuṉavē ṭāḷatti kōppāḷi
pāṇṭip piḻukkiyuṭaṉ pāmpāṭṭi-mīṇṭa
kaṭavuṭ caṭivīra mākēcaṅ kāmaṉ
makiḻcintu vāmaṉa rūpam-vikaṭaneṭum
pattiraṅ koṟṟi palakivāḷ papparappeṇ
tattacam pāram takuṇiccaṅ-kattu
muṟiyīṇ ṭiruñcittu muṇṭita maṉṉap
paṟipaṇ ṭitaṉpuṭpa pāṇam-iṟiparavu
pattaṉ kuraviyē pappaṟi kāvataṉ
pittaṉoṭu māṇi perumpiḻukki-ettuṟiyum
ēttivaruṅ kaṭkaḷi yāṇṭu viḷiyāṭṭuk
kōtta paṟikkuṭumpu kōṟkūttu-mūtta
kiḻavaṉ kiḻaviyē kiḷḷup piṟāṇṭi
aḻakuṭiya paṇṇivika ṭāṅkan-tikaḻcempoṉ
ammaṉi pantu kaḻaṅkāṭa lālikkum
viṇṇakak kāḷi viṟaṟkonti-allāta
vāynta taṉivaṇṭu vāricci picciyuṭaṉ
cānta muṭiya caṭātāri-ēyntaviṭi
takkapiṭār nirttan taḷippāṭṭuc cāturaṅkan
tokka toḻilpuṉinta cōṇāṇṭu-mikka
maliyāḷi vētāḷi vāṇi kutiri
ciliyāṭu vēṭu civapput-taliyil
tiruviḷakkup picci tirukkuṉ ṟayiṟpeṇ
ṭiruṇmukattup pēti yiruḷaṉ-porumukattup
pallāṅ kuḻiyē pakaṭi pakavatiyāḷ
nallārtan tōḷvīccu naṟcāḻal-allāta
unti yavaliṭi yūrāḷi yōkiṉicci
kuntivarum pāraṉ kuṇalikkūt-tantiyampō
tāṭuṅ kaḷikoyyu muḷḷippū viyaṉukkup
pāṭumpāṭ ṭāṭum paṭupaḷḷi-nāṭaṟiyuṅ
kumpīṭu nāṭṭaṅ kuṇāṭṭaṅ kuṇāliyē
tuñcāta cummippūc cōṉaka-mañcari
ēṟṟa vuḻimi paṟimimuta leṉṟeṇṇik
kōttavarik kūttiṉ kulam."     
 i-iryāciriyaṉ amiti
26 -36 	yāḻum kuḻalum cīrum miṭaṟum tāḻkural taṇṇumi āṭaloṭu - yāḻppāṭalum vaṅkiyap pāṭalum tāḷak kūṟupāṭukaḷum miṭaṟṟup pāṭalum mantamākiya curat tiṉiyuṭiya taṇṇumiyum kūttukkaḷum vallaṉāy, ivaṟṟiṉ icinta pāṭal iciyuṭaṉ paṭuttu - ivaṟṟuṭaṉē cērac ceyta urukkaḷi ici koḷḷumpaṭiyum cuvi poruntumpaṭiyum puṇarkkavum vallaṉāy, varikkum āṭaṟkum uripporuḷ iyakki - centuṟi veṇṭuṟi eṉṉum iruvakippaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷukkum poruḷāṉa iyakkam nāṉkiṉiyum amittu, tēcikat tiruviṉ ōci kaṭippiṭittu - aḻakuṭiya tēcāntaraṅkaḷiṉ pāṭi kaḷiyum aṟintu, tēcikat tiruviṉ ōci yellām āciṉṟu uṇarnta aṟiviṉaṉ āki - antap pāṭikaḷ icipūṇum paṭiyiyum aṟintu. kaviyatu kuṟippum āṭaṟṟokutiyum pakutip pāṭalum koḷuttuṅ kāli - iyaṟpulavaṉ karuttum nāṭakap pulavaṉ īṭuvaravukaḷum avaṟṟuk kaṭitta pāṭalkaḷum tammiṟ cantippikku miṭattu, vaciaṟu kēḷvi vakuttaṉaṉ virikkum - kuṟṟan tīrnta nūlvaḻakkālē vakukkavum virikkavum vallaṉāyuḷḷa, aciyā marapiṉ iciyōṉtāṉum - taḷarāta iyalpiṉiyuṭiya icippulavaṉum ; 
 26.		yāḻ - pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa yāḻ nālvakippaṭum. avaṟṟukku narampu muṟiyē irupattoṉṟum, pattoṉpatum, patiṉāṉkum, ēḻum ākum. eṉṉi?
"	oṉṟu mirupatu moṉpatum pattuṭaṉē niṉṟa patiṉāṉkum piṉṉēḻum-kuṉṟāta
nālvaki yāḻiṟku naṉṉarampu coṉmuṟiyē
mēlvaki nūlōr viti"
eṉpavākaliṉ. ōrēḻmēṟpattuṭaṉē eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ makara yāḻiṟku narampu patiṉēḻākum. innālvakiyaṉṟic ciṟupāṉmiyāṉ varum yāḻ piṟavu muḷa eṉpar. yāḻiṉ piṟa vilakkaṇaṅkaḷ piṉ kāṉalvari yuriyiṟ kūṟappaṭum. yāḻ- yāḻppāṭal eṉka.
kuḻal - kuḻaliṉpāṭal eṉka. kuḻal - vaṅkiyam ; atu mūṅkil, cantaṉam, veṇkalam, ceṅkāli, karuṅkāli yeṉṉum aintāṉum iyaṟṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ mūṅkilē mutaṉmiyāṉatu ; atu paṟṟiyē pullāṅkuḻal, vēyṅkuḻal, vaṅkiyam, vēṇu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ kuḻalukku vaḻaṅkuva vāyiṉa. mūṅkil uttamam, veṇkalam mattimam, ēṉiya atamam eṉpar. ikkālattuk karuṅkāli ceṅkāli cantaṉam ivaṟṟāṟ koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum, ivi koḷḷu miṭattu, uyarnta otta nilattiṟ peruka vaḷarntu nālu kāṟṟum mayaṅkiṉ nāta milliyātalāl, mayaṅkā nilattilē iḷamiyum neṭum pirāyamumiṉṟi oru puruṭāyup pukka periya maratti veṭṭi, oru puruṭākāramākac ceytu, ataṉi niḻalilē yiṭṭu āṟa vittu, tirukutal piḷattal pōḻntupaṭutal iṉmi yaṟintu, ōr yāṇṭu ceṉṟapiṉ vaṅkiyam ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, itaṉ nīḷam irupatu viralaḷavum, cuṟṟu nālari viralaḷavumām eṉavum, itu tuḷiyiṭumiṭattu nellariciyil oru pāti maraṉiṟuttik kaṭintu veṇkalattāl aṇicu paṇṇi iṭa mukatti yaṭittu valamukam veḷiyāka viṭappaṭum eṉavum, itilē tūpamukattiṉ iraṇṭu viralnīkki mutalvāyviṭṭu, immutal vāykku ēḻaṅkulamviṭṭu vaḷivāyiṉum iraṇṭu nīkki naṭuviṉiṉṟa oṉpatu viraliṉum eṭṭut tuḷiyiṭappaṭum eṉavum, tuḷikaḷiṉ iṭipparappu oru viralakalam koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum kūṟuvar. itaṉi vācikkumiṭattu, vaḷivāy cērnta tuḷiyi muttiri yeṉṟu nīkki, muṉṉiṉṟa ēḻu tuḷiyiṉum iṭakkiyiṉ iṭi mūṉṟuviralum, valakkiyiṉ peruvira loḻinta nāṉku viralum paṟṟi vācikkappaṭum. iv vaṅkiyattiṉ ēḻu tuḷikaḷilum carikamapatani eṉṉum ēḻeḻuttiṉiyum māttirippaṭuttit toḻil ceyya ivaṟṟuḷḷē ēḻiciyum piṟakkum ; ivi piṟantu ivaṟṟuḷḷē paṇkaḷ piṟakkum;
"	carika mapataniyeṉ ṟēḻeḻuttāṟ ṟāṉam
variparanta kaṇṇiṇāy vittut-terivariya
ēḻiciyun tōṉṟu mivaṟṟuḷē paṇpiṟakkum
cūḻmutalāñ cuttat tuḷi"
eṉpatu kāṇka.
cīr - akak kūttiṟkum puṟakkūttiṟku muriya iruvakit tāḷak kūṟupāṭukaḷ.
miṭaṟu - miṭaṟṟuppāṭal. itaṉic cārīravīṇi yeṉpar. puṟkalam eṉappaṭum uṭampu aimpūtaṅkaḷiṉ pariṇāmattāl ākumuṟimi aṭiyārkku nallārāl viritturikkap paṭṭuḷatu; aḵtu īṇṭikku vēṇṭappaṭuvatiṉṟu.
uṭampiṉaḷavu taṉkiyāl toṇṇūṟṟāṟu aṅkulam eṉavum, avaṟṟuḷ mēlē nāṟpattēḻari yaṅkulamum kīḻē nāṟpattēḻari yaṅkulamum viṭṭu naṭuniṉṟa ōraṅkulam mūlātāram eṉavum, mūlātāran toṭaṅki eḻuttiṉ nātam āḷattiyāyp piṉ ici yeṉṟum paṇṇeṉṟum peyarām eṉavum, neñcum miṭaṟum nākkum mūkkum aṇṇākkum utaṭum pallum taliyum eṉṉum peruntāṉam eṭṭiṉum eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital kampitam kuṭilam oli uruṭṭu tākku eṉṉuṅ kiriyika ḷeṭṭāṉum paṇṇip paṭuttalāṟ paṇṇeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu eṉavum kūṟuvar.
āḷatti yeṉpatu ikkālattu ālāpaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkap peṟum. atu makara oṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭiya kuṟṟeḻuttālum, neṭṭeḻut tālum teṉṉā, teṉā, teṉṉā teṉā eṉṉum acikaḷ kūṭṭiyum ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, maṟṟum, ma, na, ta eṉṉum mūṉṟu oṟṟukkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya a i u e o eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tintāṉum, ā ī ū ē ō eṉṉum neṭṭe.ḻut tintāṉum ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, ivvāḷattitāṉ accu, pāraṇi yeṉṟum, kāṭṭāḷatti,  paṇṇāḷatti yeṉṟum eḻuttu vēṟṟumi yāṟ peyareytum eṉavum, piṟavāṟum kūṟupa. 
 27. 		taṇṇumi - piṟakaruvikaṭkum upalakkaṇam. 
 28,		āṭal - akakkūttu, puṟakkūttu, patiṉōrāṭal eṉpaṉa. 
 29. 		urip poruḷ - iyakkam ; pāṭṭiṉ naṭi. atu mutaṉaṭi, vāram, kūṭi, tiraḷ eṉa nālvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, mutaṉaṭi mikat tāḻnta celaviṉi yuṭiyatu ; vāram colloḻukkamum ici yoḻukkamum uṭiyatu ; kūṭi coṟ ceṟivum icic ceṟivum uṭiyatu ; tiraḷ mika muṭukiya naṭiyiṉi yuṭiyatu.
iciyuṭaṉ paṭuttu iyakkik kaṭip piṭittu uṇarnta aṟi viṉaṉāki virikkum iciyōṉ eṉka. 
kaviñaṉ amiti
37 - 44 	imiḻ kaṭal varippil tamiḻakam aṟiya - olikkiṉṟa kaṭal cūḻnta puviyiṉkaṇṇē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉar aṟiya, tamiḻ muḻutu aṟinta taṉmiyaṉ āki - muttamiḻum tuṟipōkak kaṟṟuṇarnta taṉmiyi yuṭiyaṉāki, vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟu iru tiṟattiṉ - vēttiya leṉṟum potuviya leṉṟum kūṟappaṭum iraṇṭu kūṟupāṭṭiṉi yuṭiya, nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭippiṭitta - nāṭaka nūli naṉṟākap paṟṟikkoṇṭu, iciyōṉ vakkirit tiṭṭatit uṇarntu - icip pulavaṉ āḷatti vitta paṇṇīr miyi aṟintu, āṅku - aṟinta vaṇṇam, aciyā marapiṉ - taḷarāta muṟimiyālē, atu paṭavittu - avaṉ tāḷaniliyil eyta vitta niṟam taṉ kaviyilē tōṉṟa vikka vallaṉāy, māṟṟōr ceyta vacimoḻi aṟintu nāttolivu illā - muṉpakivar ceyta vaci moḻikaḷi yaṟintu avi tōṟṟātapaṭi vaci yillāta moḻikaḷāl nāṭakak kavi ceyyavalla keṭāta nāviṉiyuṭiya, naṉṉūṟ pulavaṉum - nalla nūli valla pulavaṉum ; 
37. 		tamiḻakam - vēṅkaṭam kumari tīmpuṉaṟ puvam eṉpaṉa vaṭakkum teṟkum kuṭakkum kuṇakkum elliyākavuṭiya vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇ poḻil varippu. 
41. 		vakkirittal - āḷatti ceytal. uṇarntu eṉpataṟku, mutalum muṟiyum muṭivum niṟiyum kuṟiyum kiḻamiyum valivum melivum camaṉum variyaṟiyum nīrmiyum eṉṉum patiṉoru pākupāṭṭiṉāṉum aṟintu eṉpar.
taṉmiyaṉākik kaṭippiṭittu uṇarntu vittu aṟintu tolivillāta pulavaṉum eṉka. 
taṇṇumi yāciriyaṉ amiti
45 - 55 	āṭal pāṭal iciyē tamiḻē - ellāk kūttukkaḷum ellāp pāṭṭukkaḷum ellā icikaḷum iyal ici nāṭaka meṉṉum mūvakit tamiḻkaḷum, paṇṇē pāṇi tūkkē muṭamē - ellāp paṇkaḷum iruvakit tāḷaṅkaḷum eḻuvakit tūkkukkaḷum ivaṟṟiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷum, tēcikam - iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticiccol, vaṭacol eṉṉum nālvakic col vaḻakkukkaḷum, eṉṟivi āciṉ uṇarntu - eṉṟu collappaṭṭa ivaṟṟi nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntu, kūṭi nilattik kuṟiviṉṟi mikuttu - ōruruvi iraṭṭikkiraṭṭi cērtta viṭattu nekiḻātapaṭi nirampa niṟuttavum, āṅku vāra nilatti vāṅkupu vāṅki - avviṭattup peṟum iraṭṭiyip pākavuruvāṉavaḻi niṟkumāṉam niṟuttik kaḻiyumāṉaṅ kaḻikkavum vallaṉāy, vāṅkiya vārattu yāḻum kuḻalum ēṅkiya miṭaṟum icivaṉa kēṭpa - ippaṭi nikaḻnta urukkaḷil yāḻp pāṭalum kuḻaliṉ pāṭalum kaṇṭap pāṭalum icintu naṭakkiṉṟapaṭi kēṭpōr cevik koḷḷumāṟu, kūr ukirkkaraṇam kuṟi aṟintu cērtti - taṇṇumiyi viraliṉ ceykiyālē kuṟiyaṟintu cēravācikka vallaṉāy, ākkalum aṭakkalum - maṟṟik karuvi kaḷiṉ kuṟiyi nirapputalum mikutiyi aṭakkutalum, mīt tiṟam paṭāmi - ākkumiṭattum aṭakkumiṭattum iciyil intiran tōṉṟāmaṟ ceytalum, cittirak karaṇam citi viṉṟu celuttum - ivvaṉittum ceyyumiṭattuk kit toḻil aḻaku peṟac ceytu kāṭṭalum vallaṉāy, attaku taṇṇumi aruntoḻil mutalvaṉum - attaṉmi yuṭiya taṇṇumik karuviyiṉ ariya toḻiliyuṭiya āciriyaṉum; 
45. 		ici - narappaṭivāl urikkappaṭṭa patiṉōrāyirattut toḷḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya ātiyicikaḷ eṉpar. itaṟkup piramāṇamāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭap peṟṟatōr cūttiram aṭiyil varumāṟu citintu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu;
"	uyiruyir meyyaḷa vurittavim pāliṉum uṭaṟami ḻiyalici yēḻuṭaṉ pakuttu
mūvēḻ peytan........................
toṇṭu mīṇṭa paṉṉī rāyiraṅ
koṇṭaṉa riyaṟṟal koḷivallōr kaṭaṉē"
45.		tamiḻ eṉpataṟku, vaṭaveḻuttorīi vanta eḻuttāṉē uṟaḻntu kāṭṭappaṭṭa vākkiyak kūṟukaḷ eṉṟum urippar. 
46. 		paṇ - narappaṭivāl niṟantōṉṟap paṇṇap paṭāniṉṟa paṇṇum, paṇṇiyaṟ ṟiṟamum, tiṟamum, tiṟattiṟamu mām.
47.		muṭam - kuṟṟam.
47. 		ācu - nuṇmi. āciṉ ṟuṇarntu eṉṉum pāṭat tiṟkuk kuṟṟamiṉṟi yuṇarnteṉpatu poruḷākum.; 
51.	 ēṅkiya - olittaliyuṭiya.
52.	 kūrukir - kūriya nakam; viralukku ākupeyar. karaṇam-ceyki.
53. 		taṇṇumi eṉṟatu ēṉittōṟ karuvikaḷiyum aṭakki niṉṟatu. avaṟṟi,
"	pēriki paṭakam iṭakki uṭukki
cīrmiku mattaḷam calliki karaṭiki
timili kuṭamuḻāt takki kaṇappaṟi
tamarukam taṇṇumi tāvil taṭāri
antari muḻavoṭu cantira vaḷiyam
monti muracē kaṇviṭu tūmpu
nicāḷam tuṭumi ciṟupaṟi aṭakkam
mācil takuṇiccam viralēṟu pākam
tokka upāṅkam tuṭiperum paṟiyeṉa
mikka nūlōr viritturit taṉarē"
eṉṉuñ cūttirat tālaṟika. ivi akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇami muḻavu, nāṇ muḻavu, kāli muḻavu eṉa eḻuvakippaṭum eṉṟum, muṉ coṉṉa uttamamāṉa mattaḷam calliki iṭakki karaṭiki pēriki paṭakam kuṭamuḻā eṉpaṉa akamuḻavum, mattimamāṉa taṇṇumi takki takuṇiccam mutaliyaṉa akappuṟamuḻavum, atamak karuviyāṉa kaṇappaṟi muta liyaṉa puṟamuḻavum, muṉ kūṟappaṭāta neytaṟ paṟi mutaliyaṉa puṟappuṟa muḻavum, muracu nicāḷam tuṭumi timili yeṉṉum vīramuḻavu nāṉkum paṇṇami muḻavum, nāḻikip paṟiyāṉatu nāṇmuḻavum, tuṭi eṉpatu kāli muḻavum ākumeṉṟum kūṟuvar. aṭikaḷ taṇṇumi yoṉṟaṉiyē vitantōtutaliṉ atuvē ēṉik karuvikaṭ kellām mutalāmeṉpatu peṟṟām.     
kuḻalāciriyaṉ amiti
56 - 69 	colliya iyalpiṉ - nūlkaḷiṟ coṉṉa muṟimiyālē, cittira vañcaṉi pulliya aṟintu - cittirap puṇarppum vañcaṉip puṇarppum eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉiyum aṟintu, puṇarp
pōṉ paṇpiṉ - puṇarkkavalla pāṭalāciriyaṉi yotta aṟiviṉi yuṭiyaṉāki, varttaṉi nāṉkum mayal aṟap peytu - ārōkaṇa avarōkaṇaṅkaḷil viralkaḷi viṭṭup
piṭikkum varttaṉi nāṉkiṉālum nūṟṟu mūṉṟu paṇṇīrmi kaḷiyum tannili kuliyāmaṟ kāṭṭavallaṉāy, āṅku - avviṭattu, ēṟṟiya kural iḷi eṉṟu iru narampiṉ - patiṉāṟ kōviyiṉiṭattuk kural narampu iraṭṭikkavarum arum pāliyiyum, iḷi narampu iraṭṭikkavarum mēṟcem pāliyiyum, ivipōla allāta pālikaḷiyum, oppak kēṭkum uṇarviṉaṉ āki - ici nūl vaḻakkālē iṇi narampu toṭuttup pāṭum aṟiviṉiyumuṭiyaṉāy, paṇ ami muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟi aṟintu - paṇṇutalaminta muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟiyiṉi aṟintu, taṇṇumi mutalvaṉ taṉṉoṭum porunti - taṇṇumi mutalvaṉōṭum porunti, vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭi yāḻmēl vittu āṅku - niṟat tiṉiyuṭiya iḷiyeṉṉum narampiṉi yāḻmēl vittu, iciyōṉ pāṭiya iciyiṉ iyaṟki - ataṉvaḻiyē icikkāraṉ pāṭiya pāṭṭiṉiyalpi, vantatu vaḷarttu - pāṭukiṉṟa paṇ varavukaḷukkuc curam kuṟivu paṭāmi niṟutti, varuvatu oṟṟi - antap paṇṇukku ayal viravāmal nōkki iṉpuṟa iyakki - vaṇṇa mutalākak kāṭṭappaṭṭa pāṭaliyal vaḻakkellām cuvi porunta nirampak kāṭṭi, ici paṭa vittu - muṟkūṟiya mutalum muṟiyum mutalāṉa paṇṇilakkaṇam patiṉoṉṟiṉiyum nirampa vittu, vāra nilattik kēṭiṉṟu pārttu - vārappāṭali aḷavu nirampa niṟuttavallaṉāy, āṅku - pāṭaliṭattu, īranilattiṉ eḻuttu eḻuttāka - coṉṉīrmikaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ citiyāmalē eḻutteḻuttāka icikkum, vaḻuviṉṟu kuḻalōṉ tāṉum - iccollappaṭṭa iyalpukaḷi ilakkaṇappaṭi vaḻuvāmal vācittukkāṭṭa valla kuḻalāciriyaṉum;
56. 		cittirap puṇarppāvatu icikoḷḷum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mēlē valloṟṟu vantavaḻi melloṟṟup pōlap paṇṇīrmi niṟuttal. vañcaṉip puṇarppāvatu ici koḷḷā veḻuttukkaḷiṉ mēlē valloṟṟu vantavaḻi melloṟṟuppōla nekiḻttup puṇarttal.
58.		varttaṉi - kuḻalil viraluḷarntūtun tuḷikaḷ cuṭṭuviral mutalāka viṭṭup piṭippatu ārōkaṇam; ciṟu viral mutalāka viṭṭuppiṭippatu avarōkaṇam; iṅṅaṉam ēṟṟiyiṟakkalum, iṟakkiyēṟṟalum varttaṉi yeṉappaṭum.
59. 		‘ēṟṟiya kuraliḷi............uṇarviṉaṉāki' eṉpataṉ poruḷ piṉṉar yāḻāciriyaṉ amiti kūṟumiṭattu urikku muṟiyāl viḷakkamām.
63. paṭṭaṭi - aṭimaṇi; ellāp paṇṇiṟkum aṭi maṇiyātaliṉ iḷiyeṉṉum narampu, paṭṭaṭi yeṉap paṭṭatu. yāḻiṉ mēṟpaṇkaḷi iḷi muṟiyālē vittu eṉka. iḷi muṟiyāvatu caṭcak kiramam.  āṅku;aci.
65. oṟṟi - niṉintu.
67. 		vāra nilattik kēṭiṉṟu pārttu eṉpatu - mutaṉaṭi, vāram, kūṭi, tiraḷ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa iyakkam nāṉkiṉum mutal naṭi mikavum tāḻnta celaviṉi yuṭittākalāṉum, tiraḷmikamuṭukiya naṭiyiṉi yuṭittākalāṉum ivi tavirntum, iṭippaṭṭa vārap pāṭal colloḻukkamum iciyoḻukkamum uṭittākalāṉum, kūṭippāṭal coṟceṟivum icicceṟivum uṭittākalāṉum ciṟappunōkki, avviraṇṭiṉuḷḷum vārappāṭali aḷavu nirampa niṟuttavallaṉāy eṉka. eṉavē, kūṭippāṭalum amivatāyiṟṟu.
69.		icikkum eṉpati eḻutteḻuttāka icikkum eṉṟum, collappaṭṭa yāvaṟṟiyum vaḻuviṉṟicikkum eṉṟum pirittuk kūṭṭuka.
aṟintu mayalaṟappeytu uṇarviṉaṉāki aṟintu porunti vittu vaḷarttu oṟṟi iyakki vittup pārttu icikkuṅ kuḻa lōṉ eṉka.
yāḻāciriyaṉ amiti
ippakutiyiṉ uri naṉku viḷaṅkutaṟ poruṭṭu iciyip paṟṟiya cila koḷkikaḷi mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkutum;-
tamiḻil ici eṉpatu curam, irākam eṉṉum iru poruḷilum vaḻaṅkum. irākattiṟku iciyeṉṉum peyaraṉṟip paṇ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. vaṭamoḻiyil curutiyeṉṟu collap paṭuvatu tamiḻil alaku eṉṟum, māttiri yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. maṟṟum, curam eṉpataṟku narampu eṉṟa peyarum, paṇ eṉpataṟku yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum. ēḻicikaṭkum tamiḻilē kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi,  uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉpaṉa peyarkaḷām. ēḻu curaṅkaṭkum vaṭamoḻiyilē caṭcam, riṭapam kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, tivatam, niṭātam eṉpaṉa peyarkaḷām.
tamiḻil ēḻicikaṭkum irupattiraṇṭu alakukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avi kural mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē 4, 4, 3, 2, 4, 3, 2 ākum; itaṉi,
"	kuraltuttam nāṉku kiḷimūṉ ṟiraṇṭām  kuriyā uḻiyiḷi nāṉku - viriyā
viḷariyeṉiṉ mūṉṟiraṇṭu tārameṉac coṉṉār 
kaḷaricēr kaṇṇuṟ ṟavar"
eṉpataṉāṉaṟika.
vaṭamoḻiyilum ēḻu curaṅkaṭkum irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avi caṭcam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṟiyē 4, 3, 2, 4, 4, 3, 2 ākum.
inta ici allatu curaṅkaḷiṉ varicikkut tamiḻil kōvi yeṉṟum peyaruṇṭu. ivvarici yamippukkaḷ āyappāli, caturappāli, vaṭṭappāli, tirikōṇappāli eṉa nālvakip paṭukiṉṟaṉa, ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāvatu irākam allatu paṇ ākum.
ēḻici yeṉappaṭum curaṅkaḷ ēḻiṉiyum māṟuntiṟat tiṉālēyē palavakip paṇkaḷ allatu irākaṅkaḷ uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa; ākaliṉ curaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi mutalil naṉku terintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. kural mutalāya ēḻaṉuḷ, tārattil uḻiyum, uḻiyiṟ kuralum, kuralil iḷiyum, iḷiyil tuttamum, tuttattil viḷariyum, viḷariyiṟ kikkiḷiyum  piṟakkum; ivaṟṟuḷ mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟiyatu tāram.
"	tārattuṭ ṭōṉṟum uḻiyuḻi yuṭṭōṉṟum
ōruṅ kural kurali ṉuṭṭōṉṟic - cērumiḷi
yuṭṭōṉṟun tuttattuṭ ṭōṉṟum viḷariyuṭ
kikkiḷi tōṉṟum piṟappu"
eṉpatu kāṇka. tārattuḷ ataṟ kintāvatākiya uḻiyum, uḻiyuḷ ataṟ kintāvatākiya kuralum, immuṟiyē ēṉiyavum tōṉṟiṉa veṉpatu aṟiyaṟpālatu. ivvēḻicikaḷum vaṭṭap
pāli muṟiyil ōr irāci maṇṭalattiṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu irukki kaḷiyum iṭamākak koḷḷum eṉpatu piṉṉar āycciyar kuraviyuḷ viḷakkappaṭum.
ōr irākam allatu paṇṇiṉi uṇṭākkutaṟ poruṭṭu mutalilē toṭaṅkappeṟum curam kural eṉpatākum. ēḻu curaṅkaḷil etaṉiyum kuralāka niṟuttutal uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu kuralē kuralākavum, tuttam kuralākavum, kikkiḷi kuralākavum iṅṅaṉam toṭaṅkap peṟum eṉpatām.
curaṅkaḷik koṇṭu iciyi eḻupputaṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ carikamapatani eṉṉum ēḻumām. ivveḻuttukkaḷ caṭcam mutaliya peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu koḷvaṉa poruntāmiyāl, ivi kuṟiyīṭāka amittuk koḷḷap peṟṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum. vaṭa nūṟṟuṟipōyacilarum iṅṅaṉam karutuvar.
"	ā ī ū ē ai ō oḷa veṉum 
ivvē ḻeḻuttum ēḻicik kuriya"
eṉat tivākaram kūṟutaliṉ, ā mutaliya neṭṭuyir ēḻaṉiyum karuviyākak koṇṭu paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ ici pāṭiṉa rātalum vēṇṭum.
tamiḻilē irākaṅkaḷ paṇ eṉavum, tiṟam eṉavum iru vakip paṭum. "niṟinaram piṟṟē paṇṇeṉa lākum," "kuṟinaram piṟṟē tiṟameṉap paṭumē" eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpākkaḷāl ēḻunarampāṉum iyaṉṟatu paṇṇām eṉpatum, āṟu aintu nāṉku eṉak kuṟinta narampukaḷāṉ iyaṉṟaṉa tiṟamām eṉpatum peṟap paṭum. tiṟatti mūṉṟu vakippaṭuttup paṇṇiyaṟṟiṟam, tiṟam, tiṟattiṟam eṉa vaḻaṅkutalu muṇṭu. paṇṇum, tiṟamūṉṟum ākiya in nālvaki irākaṅkaḷiyum kuṟikkum vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ campūraṇam, cāṭavam, oḷaṭavam, caturttam eṉpaṉa. innāṉkum tamiḻil paṇ, tiṟam eṉa iraṇṭākak kūṟappaṭuta lōṭu, yāvum paṇṇeṉṟē kūṟappaṭutalum uṇṭu.
tamiḻil aintiṇikkumuriya paṇkaḷ kuṟiñciyāḻ, pāli yāḻ, mulliyāḻ, neytalyāḻ, marutayāḻ eṉpaṉa, ivaṟṟuḷ neytal yāḻukku viḷari eṉpatum peyar. itu tiṟaṉil yāḻ eṉap paṭutaliṉ, tiṟaṅkaḷuṭiya ēṉi nāṉkumē perumpaṇkaḷ eṉappaṭum; "yāma yāḻppeyar kuṟiñci yāḻum, cevvaḻi yāḻp peyar mulli yāḻum, pāli yāḻum maruta yāḻumeṉa, nālvaki yāḻum nāṟperum paṇṇē" eṉpatu tivākaram; itiliruntu kuṟiñci yāḻukku yāma yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum, mulli yāḻukkuc cevvaḻi yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭeṉpatu pulaṉām. in nāṟ perum paṇṇum piṟakkumāṟu "tārat tuḻitōṉṟap pāli yāḻ taṇkural, orumuḻi tōṉṟak kuṟiñciyāḻ - nērē, iḷikuraliṟ ṟōṉṟa marutayāḻ tuttam, iḷiyiṟ piṟakkaneyta liyāḻ" eṉakkūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. itil neytal yāḻ eṉṟatu cevvaḻi yātal vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāram kuralāka uḻi ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu pāliyāḻ eṉavum, uḻikuralākak kural ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu kuṟiñciyāḻ eṉavum kural kuralāka iḷi ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu marutayāḻ eṉavum, iḷi kuralākat tuttam ataṟkuk kiḷiyākat tōṉṟuvatu cevvaḻi yāḻ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum eṉka. kiḷiyāvatu niṉṟa narampiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟum narampu; niṉṟa narampiṟku aintāvatu narampu. ivvāṟē niṉṟa narampiṟku nāṉkāvatu naṭpu narampeṉṟum, mūṉ ṟāvatum āṟāvatum paki narampeṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatum ēḻāvatum iṇi narampeṉṟum aṟika.
iṉi. muṟkuṟitta nāṉku paṇkaḷuḷ pāliyāḻiliruntu cempāli, paṭumalip pāli, cevvaḻip pāli, arumpāli, kōṭippāli, viḷarip pāli, mēṟcem pāli eṉṉum ēḻu pāli yicikaḷ piṟakkum; ivi piṟakkumāṟu mēl ik kāti yuḷḷum, āycciyar kuraviyuḷḷum viḷakkamām.
iṉi, pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi yeṉṉum nāṟ perumpaṇkaḷil ovvoṉṟum akanili, puṟanili, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṉum iṉa vēṟu pāṭṭāl nannāṉku ākap perumpaṇkaḷ patiṉāṟākum. nāṟperum paṇkaḷuḷḷē pāli yāḻukku aintum, kuṟiñci yāḻukku eṭṭum, maruta yāḻukku nāṉkum, cevvaḻi yāḻukku nāṉkum āka irupattoru tiṟaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. "īriru paṇṇum eḻumūṉṟu tiṟaṉum" eṉpatu piṅkalam. tiṟam irupat toṉṟum akanili, puṟanili, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṉum vēṟu pāṭṭāl eṇpattu nāṉku ākum. eṉavē, paṇ patiṉāṟum, tiṟam eṇpattu nāṉkum cērntu nūṟu eṉṉum tokiyiṉavākiṉṟaṉa. piṅkala nikaṇṭilē tiṟaṅkaḷiṉ vaki yāvaṟṟiṟkum peyar kūṟi muṭitta piṉ, "tārap paṇṭiṟam piyuḷ kāñci, paṭumali yivi nūṟṟu mūṉṟu tiṟattaṉa" eṉṟu kūṟap paṭṭiruttaliṉ, tārap paṇṭiṟam, piyuḷ kāñci, paṭumali eṉṉum mūṉṟu tiṟaṅkaḷum muṟkūṟiya nūṟṟuṭaṉ cērap paṇkaḷ nūṟṟu mūṉṟu eṉṉum tokipeṟum. paṇ nūṟṟumūṉṟeṉṟal peru vaḻakku. perum paṇkaḷiṉ vaki patiṉāṟaṉuṭ paṉṉiraṇṭukkum, tiṟaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ vakiyumākiya eṇpattu nāṉkiṟkum peyar piṅkala nikaṇṭiṟ kāṇka.
iṉi, tamiḻilē kural mutalākavum, vaṭamoḻiyilē caṭcam mutalākavum ēḻucuraṅkaḷum peyar kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, kuralum caṭcamum oṉṟeṉavum, tuttamum riṭapamum oṉṟeṉavum, ivvāṟē ēṉiyavum muṟiyē ovvoṉṟā meṉavum karututal kūṭum. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟukkuk kūṟappaṭum alakum oliyum piṟappiṭamum vēṟupaṭutaliṉ, ammuṟiyē ivviraṇṭum oṉṟeṉal amiyāmi peṟṟām. īṇṭu alakiṉi nōkkutum :-
kural- 4, tuttam- 4, kikkiḷi- 3, uḻi- 2, iḷi-4, viḷari-3, tāram - 2.
  caṭcam - 4, riṭapam - 3, kāntāram - 2, mattimam - 4, pañcamam - 4, tivatam - 3, niṭātam - 2.
ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum nāṉkāvatum māttiriyil ovvāmi kāṇka. caṭcam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṅkē kāṭṭiya curutikaḷiṉ aḷavē cāraṅkatēvar iyaṟṟiya caṅkītaratṉākaram mutaliya nūṟkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇap paṭutaliṉ, ammuṟiyē tamiḻukkum poruttamātal vēṇṭum. kural eṉpataṉic caṭcam eṉṟu koḷḷātu mattimam eṉak koḷḷiṉ iraṇṭilum curutiyaḷavukaḷ ottu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kural - 4, tuttam - 4, kikkiḷi -3, uḻi - 2, iḷi-4, viḷari - 3, tāram - 2.
ma - 4, pa - 4, ta - 3, ni - 2, ca - 4, ri - 3, ka - 2.
curukkaṅkaruti mattimam mutaliya peyarkaḷ ma mutaliya eḻuttuk kaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēlē kāṭṭiyavaṟṟil iru tiṟattum alaku ottiruttal kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl paṇṭit tamiḻ ici nūlōr vaṭanūṉ muṟiyilaminta mattimatti ārampa curamākak koṇṭaṉa reṉpatu pōtarum. eṉavē, tārattu uḻi tōṉṟum eṉṟa muṟippaṭi kāntārattil niṭātamum, niṭātattil mattimamum, mattimattil caṭcamum, caṭcattil pañcamamum, pañcamattil riṭapamum, riṭapattil tivatamum piṟakkum eṉak koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟi naṉku niṉivil vittuk koḷḷiṉ, piṉ kūṟappaṭum iciyiyalpukaḷ perumpālum mayakkaṟa viḷaṅkum. mika nuṭpamāṉa iciyiyalpukaḷi aṟiya viḻivōr ici nūṟkaḷik kaṟṟum, icippayiṟci ceytum aṟital vēṇṭum.
70 -94 	(īrēḻtoṭutta............pulami yōṉuṭaṉ)
70. 		īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟik kēḷviyiṉ - cevviya muṟiyē iraṇṭēḻākat toṭukkappaṭṭa āyap pāliyāy niṉṟa patiṉāṟkōviyil, 
71. 		ōrēḻ pāli niṟuttal vēṇṭi - cempāli paṭumalip pāli cevvaḻippāli arumpāli kōṭippāli viḷarip pāli mēṟcempāli yeṉappaṭṭa ēḻu pāliyiṉiyum iṇinarampu toṭuttu nirampa niṟuttik kāṭṭal kāraṇamāka, 
72.		vaṇmiyiṟ kiṭanta tārapākamum - ippāliyiṉ muṭivut tāṉamāy valinta nilimiyiṉiyuṭiya tāram peṟṟa iraṇṭalakil ōralakiyum, 
 73.		meṉmiyiṟ kiṭanta kuraliṉ pākamum - mutaṟṟāṉamāy meliviṉiṟkum kural narampupeṟṟa nālalakil iraṇṭalakiyum, 
74. 		tārattiṟkuk kikkiḷi iṭamuṟiyāl aintāvatākaliṉ kiḷi narampeṉṟār. kiḷi - aintāvatu narampu.
 74 - 75	meykkiḷi narampiṟ kikkiḷi koḷḷak kikkiḷi - tāranarampil antarak kōlilē kikkiḷiyāka niṟuttat tārantāṉ kikkiḷiyāyiṟṟu; 
75 - 77 	oḻinta pākamum poṟpuṭit taḷarāttāram viḷarikku īttuk kiḷivaḻip paṭṭaṉaḷ - taḷarāta aḻakuṭiya tāra narampil oḻinta ōralakiyum viḷarikkuttara avviḷari tutta narampāyiṟṟu; 
77 - 78 	āṅkē kiḷiyum taṉ kiḷi aḻivu kaṇṭu avaḷ vayiṟ cēra - ammuṟiyē iḷiyum taṉ kiḷiyākiya kuraliṉ aḻiviṉik kaṇṭu ataṉpāṟ cēravum, 
79.  		ēṉi makaḷirum kiḷivaḻic cēra - ēṉiya uḻi mutalāyiṉavum tattamakkuk kiḷiyāyiṉavaṟṟiṟ cēravum ivvāṟāya patiṉāṟ kōviyilē, 
80 - 81 	mēlatu uḻi yiḷi kīḻatu kikkiḷi vampuṟu marapiṟ cempāli āyatu - uḻimutalākak kikkiḷi yiṟutiyāka melivu nāṉkum camam ēḻum valivu mūṉṟumāyp putumiyuṟṟa muṟiyālē uḻi kuralākac cempāliyāyatu; 
82 - 83 	iṟuti ātiyāka āṅkavi peṟumuṟi vanta peṟṟiyiṉ nīṅkātu - iṟutiyāy niṉṟa kikkiḷi mutalākavuḷḷa kikkiḷi tuttam kural eṉṉumavi tām tōṉṟiya iṭa muṟiyāṉa iyalpiṉ nīṅkātu, 
 84 - 85 	paṭumali cevvaḻi arumpāli eṉak kural kuralākat taṟkiḻami tirintapiṉ - kikkiḷi kuralākap paṭumalip pāliyum tuttamkuralākac cevvaḻip pāliyum kural kuralāka arumpāliyum eṉa muṟiyē tirintapiṉ, 
86. 		muṉṉataṉ vakiyē muṟimiyil tirintu āṅku - muṉ piṟpaṭiyē muṟimiyiṉ vēṟupaṭṭu,
 87. 		iḷi mutalākiya etirpaṭu kiḻamiyum - tāram viḷari iḷi eṉpaṉavum,
88. 		kōṭi viḷari mēṟcempāli eṉa - tāramkuralākak kōṭippāliyum viḷarikuralāka viḷarippāliyum iḷi kuralāka mēṟcempāliyum eṉat tiriya,
89. 		nīṭikkiṭanta kēḷvik kiṭakkiyiṉ - neṭitāyk kiṭanta curaṅkaḷiṉ iṭattē,
90. 		iṇi narampuṭiyaṉa aṇivuṟak koṇṭu āṅku - mutalum iṟutiyumāka vuḷḷa narampukaḷip poruntak koṇṭu,
91. 		yāḻ mēṟpāli iṭamuṟi meliya - yāḻiṉiṭattu arumpāli mutalāyiṉa iṭamuṟi meliyavum,
92. 		kuḻalmēṟ kōṭi valamuṟi meliya - kuḻaliṉiṭattuk kōṭippāli mutalāyiṉa valamuṟi meliyavum,
93-94	valivum melivuñ camaṉum ellām poliyak kōtta pulamiyōṉuṭaṉ - valivum melivum camaṉum viḷaṅkavum narappaṭivu keṭāmalum paṇṇīrmi mutalāyiṉa kuṉṟā malum eḻuttukkaḷāl ici ceyya valla yāḻāciriyaṉum; 
95. 		kēḷvi - narampu; curam kural mutalāya ēḻu narampiṉiyum iraṭṭitta patiṉāṟkōvi yeṉka.
96.		tārattiṟkuk kikkiḷi iṭamuṟiyāl aintā vatākaliṉ kiḷi narampeṉṟār. kiḷi - aintāvatu narampu. 
97.		viḷarikkut tuttam ammuṟiyē aintāvatākaliṉ kiḷi vaḻippaṭṭaṉaḷ eṉṟār. kiḷiyum - iḷiyum; kuralukku iḷi aintāvatākaliṉ ataṉik kiḷiyeṉṉum peyarāṟ kūṟiṉār; aṉṟi, iḷi yeṉpatē eḻutuvōrāṟ kiḷiyeṉattiripuṟṟa teṉalumām. arumpata vuriyāciriyar coṟkaḷi eñcā teṭuttup poruḷkūṟi muṭikkuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyarallar; maṟṟu, aṭiyārkku nallārē aṅṅaṉam urikkuṅ kaṭappāṭṭiṉar. āyiṉ, avar ekkāraṇattāṉō icippakutiyil muṉṉavaruritta vaṟṟiyē tāmum urittu, mūlattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ pala vaṟṟiṟkup poruḷum muṭipum terikkātu pōyiṉar. ataṉāl, iññāṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ poruḷ aṟital aritāyiṉamiyiṉ eḻutuvōrālum, patippippōrālum vaḻukkaḷ nikaḻtal iyalpēyākum.
arumpatavuriyilum, aṭiyārkku nallāruriyilum muṟiyē `ikkiramattiṉālē,' `iḷik kiramattālē' (cilap. araṅ. aṭi. 63-4) eṉavum, `patiṉāṟkōvi polintu,' `patiṉāṟ kōvi kōliṉitu' (mēṟpaṭi aṭi. 70) eṉavum `varappaṭṭa pāli,' `vaṭṭap pāli' (mēṟpaṭi aṭi. 72 - 3) eṉavum iṅṅaṉam eṇṇiṟantaṉa tirintu, tiruttam peṟāmalē muntiya patippukkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇappaṭutali nōkkiṉ mūlapāṭam urucci tintu vaḻuppaṭṭiruttal aritaṉ ṟeṉpatu pulaṉām. 
77-79	narampukaḷi makaḷirāka uruvakappaṭutti, 'vaḻippaṭṭaṉaḷ' eṉavum, `avaḷ vayiṉ' eṉavum, `ēṉi makaḷirum' eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār. itaṉi aṟiyamāṭṭātu, uriyilē `peṇṭirk kuriyatāṉa muṭiya' eṉa vantiruppatu koṇṭu, itu peṇṭirkaḷ iṉitu pāṭutaṟkēṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉārumuḷar. kiḷivaḻiccēra nīṭikkiṭanta kēḷvikkiṭakkiyiṉ iṇinarampuṭiyaṉa aṇivuṟak koṇṭu eṉa iyikka.
cemmuṟik kēḷviyākum patiṉāṟkōvi varumāṟu:
(1) kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram;
(2) kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram;
itiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāya kēḷvik kiṭakkiyākum patiṉāṟ kōvi varumāṟu:
(1)  uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram,kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, 
(2) uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram,kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, ivi muṟiyē,
ma pa ta ni cari ka - ma pa ta ni ca ri ka eṉavum
ni ca ri ka ma pa ta - ni ca ri ka ma pa ta eṉavumkūṟik koḷka.
80. 		cemmuṟi māṟivanta patiṉāṟ kōviyil uḻi mutalum kikkiḷi iṟutiyumāy niṟṟal kāṇka. 'uḻiyiḷi' eṉṉum pāṭam vaḻuppaṭṭatākal vēṇṭum; īṇṭu iḷi eṉpataṟkup poruḷoṉṟu miṉmiyum, paḻiya urikaḷil iccol vaṟitē viṭappaṭṭiruntaluṅ kāṇka.
82. 		iṟuti ātiyāka - iṟutiyākavuḷḷa kikkiḷi mutalāka. kikkiḷi iṟutiyil niṟṟal kāṇka.
85. 		kural kuralāka eṉpatu oṉṟoṉṟāka eṉpatupōl niṉṟa aṭukku. taṟkiḻami - muṟi niraṉiṟi.
87. 		etirpaṭukiḻami - etir niraṉiṟi.
90. 		iṇinarampu - mutalum iṟutiyumākavuḷḷa narampu; uḻi mutaṟ kikkiḷi yiṟutiyum, kikkiḷi mutal tuttam iṟutiyum, ivvāṟē ēṉiyavum mutalum iṟutiyumāki yeṉka; iraṭṭitta narampu eṉṟalumām. uḻi, kikkiḷi, tuttam, kural, tāram, viḷari, iḷiyeṉa iṭamuṟiyāl ovvoṉṟum kuralākac cempāli mutalāyiṉa tōṉṟiṉami aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. ivvēḻu paṇkaḷum piṟa muṟiyāl uṇṭāvaṉa vēṉiṟ kātiyilum āycciyar kuraviyilum aṟiyappaṭum. 
 91-2.	kuḻalmēṟ kōṭi eṉṟataṉāl yāḻmēl arumpāli yeṉa varuvikkappaṭṭatu. melital - iṟaṅkutal; avarōkaṇam. 
 93.		valivu - mēl ; uccam ; tāram. melivu - kīḻ ; 
	mantam. camam - mattimam. ivi ōciyiṉ mūvaki iyakkam. āṅkavi, tirintāṅku, koṇṭāṅku eṉpavaṟṟil āṅku aci. niṟuttal vēṇṭip poliyak kōtta pulamiyōṉ eṉa muṭikka. 
araṅkiṉ amiti
95-113.	(eṇṇiya......araṅkattu) 
		eṇṇiya nūlōr iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻātu maṇṇakam oruvaḻi vakuttaṉarkoṇṭu - eṇṇappaṭṭa ciṟpa nūlāciriyar vakutta iyalpukaḷiṉ vaḻuvāta vaki araṅkiyaṟṟutaṟkuk kuṟṟam nīṅkiṉa ōriṭat tilē nilam vakuttukkoṇṭu, puṇṇiya neṭuvarip pōkiya neṭuṅkaḻi kaṇṇiṭi orucāṇ vaḷarntatu koṇṭu - potiyiṉ mutalāya tippiya malippakkaṅkaḷilē nīṇṭu vaḷarnta mūṅkiliṟ kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṭi oru cāṇāka vaḷarntatu koṇṭu, nūlneṟi marapiṉ - nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum muṟiyālē, araṅkam aḷakkum kōl aḷavu irupattu nāl viralāka - araṅkam iyaṟṟutaṟku aḷakkuṅkōl uttamaṉ kip peruviral irupattu nāṉku koṇṭa aḷaviṉatāka naṟukki, eḻukōl akalattu eṇkōl nīḷattu orukōl uyarattu uṟuppiṉatāki - akkōlāl eḻukōl akalamum eṇ kōl nīḷamum orukōṟ kuṟaṭṭuyaramum uṭiyatāy, uttarap palakiyōṭu araṅkiṉ palaki vitta iṭinilam nāṟ kōl āka - tūṇiṉmītu vitta uttarap palakikkum araṅkiṉ taḷattiṟkiṭṭa palakikkum iṭiniṉṟa nilam nāṉku kōlaḷaviṉatākavum, ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭu uṭaṉ poliya - avvaḷavukaṭkup porunta vakukkappaṭṭa vāyil iraṇṭu viḷaṅkavum, tōṟṟiya araṅkil - ceyyappaṭṭa araṅkilē, toḻutaṉar ēttap pūtari eḻuti mēlnili vittu - nāl vaki varuṇap pūtariyum eḻuti yāvarum pukaḻntu vaṇaṅka mēṉilattē vittu, tūṇniḻaṟ puṟappaṭa māṇ viḷakku eṭuttu āṅku - tūṇkaḷiṉ niḻal nāyakap pattiyiṉkaṇṇum aviyiṉkaṇṇum paṭātapaṭi māṭcimippaṭṭa niliviḷakku niṟutti, orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyum karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum purintu uṭaṉvakuttu āṅku - iṭattūṇ nili yiṭattē uruvu tiriyāka orumuka veḻiṉiyum iraṇṭuvalat tūṇiṭattum uruvu tirivākap porumuka veḻiṉiyum mēṟ kaṭṭut tiriyākak karantuvara leḻiṉiyum toḻiṟpāṭṭuṭaṉē vakuttu, ōviya vitāṉattu - cittira vitāṉattiyum amittu, uripeṟu nittilattu mālit tāmam vaḷivuṭaṉ nāṟṟi - pukaḻaminta muttumālikaḷāṟ cariyum tūkkum tāma mumākat toṅkaviṭṭu, viruntupaṭak kiṭanta aruntoḻil araṅkattu - putumi yuṭittākap poruntiya ariyatoḻili yuṭiya araṅkiṉkaṇ; 
 95 - 99 	nūlōr - nāṭaka nūlōr eṉpar arumpatavuri yāciriyar. ivvulakil nūlvaḻi araṅku ceyyumiṭattut teyvattāṉamum paḷḷiyum antaṇarirukkiyum kiṇaṟum kuḷaṉum kāvum mutalāyiṉa aḻiyāta iyalpiṉiyuṭit tāy, niṟuk kappaṭṭa kuḻippuḻuti kuḻikkottu, kallappaṭṭa maṇ iṉiya nāṟṟamum iṉiya cuviyum uṭiyatāy, tāṉum tiṇṇi tāy, eṉpum umiyum paraluñ cērnta nilam, kaḷittari, uvarttari, īḷittari, pollāc cāmpaṟṟari, poṭit tari eṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa oḻintu, ūriṉ naṭuvaṇatāki yuḷḷa nilattilē tērōṭum vītikaḷ etirmukamākak koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar.
nilam vaṉpāl, meṉpāl, iṭippāleṉṟu mūvakip paṭum ; avaṟṟuḷ vaṉpālāvatu tōṇṭappaṭṭa kuḻiyil am maṇṇiṉip peyyiṉ kuḻiyiṉmaṇ mikuvatu ; meṉpālāvatu kuṟivatu ; iṭippālāvatu ottiruppatu. iṭippālē vēṇṭappaṭuvatām. maṇṇiṉ cuvikaḷuḷ tuvarppu accamum, puḷippu nōyum, kāḻppup pacinīṭutalum, kippuk kēṭum, uvarppuk kalakkamum, tittippu aṉpum viḷikkumākaliṉ tittippē koḷḷat takuvatām. 
 97. 		pōkiya - nīṇṭa.
100.		uttamarāvār : mikka neṭumiyum mikka kuṟumiyumil lōr. aṇu eṭṭuk koṇṭatu tērt tukaḷ ; tērt tukaḷ eṭṭukkoṇṭatu immi ; immi eṭṭukkoṇṭatu eḷḷu; eḷḷu eṭṭuk koṇṭatu nellu; nellu eṭṭuk koṇṭatu peruviralām.
105.		vāyil iraṇṭāvaṉa : uṭpukavum puṟañcellavum camittavi. maṟṟum, karantu pōkkiṭaṉum, kūttar kuṭiñip paḷḷiyum, araṅkamum, ataṉetir maṉṉar māntarōṭirukkum aviyaraṅkamum, puviniṟi māntar poruntiya kōṭṭiyum mutalāyiṉa koḷḷappaṭum.
 107. 	pūtar - varuṇap pūtar ; ivar vacciratēkaṉ, vacciratantaṉ, varuṇaṉ, irattakēcuvaraṉ eṉṉum nālvarumām. nāl vaki varuṇattāriṉ uruvam toḻil mutaliyaṉa viḷaṅka iṅṅaṉam varuṇappūtam nāṉkum vakuppaṭṭaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉiyalpu piṉṉar aḻaṟpaṭu kātiyuḷ viritturikkap paṭum. 
 111.	 	uri - pukaḻ. mēl ūrkāṇ kātiyil 1"cantira kuruvē yaṅkā rakaṉeṉa, vanta nīrmiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum" eṉap pākupaṭutturikkappaṭum pukaḻ eṉka. 
vakuttaṉar koṇṭuvittu eṭuttu vakuttu nāṟṟikkiṭanta araṅkatteṉka.
talikkōl amiti
114 - 128    (pērici maṉṉar ... vittāṅku) pērici maṉṉar peyarpuṟattu eṭutta - periya pukaḻiyuṭiya pakiyaracar pōrceytu puṟaṅkoṭuttavaḻip paṟikkappaṭṭa, cīr iyal veṇ kuṭik kāmpu naṉi koṇṭu - aḻaku poruntiya veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyiṉ kāmpi naṉku eṭuttu kaṇiṭi navamaṇi oḻukki maṇṇiya - kaṇukkaḷ tōṟum kaḻuviya nava maṇikaḷāṟ kaṭṭi, nāvalam polam takaṭṭu iṭinilam pōkki - cāmpūnatam eṉṉum poṉṉiṉ takaṭṭālē kaṇuk kaṭku naṭuvākiya iṭaṅkaḷik kaṭṭi, kāval veṇkuṭi maṉṉavaṉ kōyil - ulakiṉip purakkum veṇkuṭiyi yuṭiya aracaṉ kōyilil, intira ciṟuvaṉ cayantaṉ ākeṉa - tēvēntiraṉ makaṉ cayantaṉāka niṉittu, vantaṉi ceytu vaḻi paṭu talikkōl - mantira vitiyālē pūcittu vaḻipaṭṭuk kāppamittu iruttiya talikkōli, puṇṇiya naṉṉīr poṟ kuṭattu ēnti maṇṇiya piṉṉar māli aṇintu - muṉkūṟip pōnta āṭalāciriyaṉ mutalāyiṉōr puṇṇiya natikaḷiṉ nalla nīrip poṟ kuṭattilē mukantu vantu nīrāṭṭiya piṉpu mālikaḷuñ cūṭṭi, nalam taru nāḷāl - itaṟkup poruntiya nalla nāḷilē, polam pūṇ ōṭi aracu uvāt taṭakkiyil paraciṉar koṇṭu - poṉṉālākiya pūṇiṉiyum paṭṭattiṉiyu muṭiya paṭṭa varuttaṉattiṉ periya kiyil vāḻttuṭaṉ koṭuttu ataṉi uṭaṉkoṇṭu, muracu eḻuntu iyampa - mummuracum eḻuntu muḻaṅkavum, pal iyam ārppa - avi yaṉṟip pala vācciyaṅkaḷum olikkavum, aracoṭu paṭṭa aim peruṅ kuḻuvum - aracaṉum amiccar purōkitar cēṉāpatiyar tūtuvar cāraṇar eṉṉum aimperuṅ kuḻuviṉarum uṭaṉvara, tēr valam ceytu kavi kikkoṭuppa - vītiyiṉ kaṇ niṉṟa tēruṭaṉ valañceytu tērmici niṉṟa pāṭuvōṉ kiyilē it talikkōlik koṭuttu, ūrvalañ ceytu pukuntu muṉ vittāṅku - nakariyi valamākavantu araṅkiṉ kaṭpukuntu talikkōli etirmukamāka vittapiṉ;
114 - 115	talikkōl koḷḷumiṭattu māṟṟaracar kuṭik kāmpum, paki vēntaratu eyiṟpuṟattu veṭṭiṉa mūṅkilum, puṇṇiya variyiṉ mūṅkilum ām eṉpar ; ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉiya iraṇṭum vēttiyaṟkum, piṉṉiyatu potu viyaṟku mām. 
116 		maṇṇiya-kaḻuviya ;maṇṇiya maṇi yeṉka. 
117. 		nāvalam polam - cāmpūnatam eṉṉum poṉ. nāval- campu; am cāriyi.. 1"nāvaloṭu peyariya polampuṉi yaviriḻi" eṉṟār āciriyar nakkīraṉārum. 
118 - 120 	maṉṉavaṉ kōyilil kāppamittu iruttiya veṉa viritturikka. ākeṉa - āka. cayantaṉ akattiyar cāpattāl mūṅkilāyp piṟantu talikkōṟ ṟāṉattuc cāpam nīṅkiṉā ṉākaliṉ talikkōlic cayantaṉāka niṉittu eṉṟār. 
123. 		nāḷāl, vēṟṟumi mayakkam. pūṇ - kimpuri ; aṇi kalamumām.
"	pūrāṭaṅ kārttiki pūram paraṇikalam
cīrā tiriyaviṭṭañ cittiriyō-ṭārumuṟam
māci yiṭapam arituli vāṉkaṭakam
pēciya tēḷ mituṉam pēcutu"
eṉṉum mativāṇaṉār kūṟṟāl naṉmiyākiya nāḷ pattum irāci āṟum aṟika. 
124. 		aracuvā - paṭṭavaruttaṉam ; paṭṭattiyāṉi. āṭalāciriyaṉ mutalāṉōr talikkōli yāṉi kiyiṟ koṭuttut tēruṭaṉ valañ ceytu, piṉ kaviyiṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttu ūrvalañ ceytu pukuntu vittapiṉ eṉka.
mātavi araṅkiṟ pukuntu āṭukiṉṟa viyalpu
129 - 159 	(iyalpiṉiṉ......kāṭṭiṉa ḷātaliṉ) iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāa irukki muṟimiyiṉ - aracaṉ mutaliya yāvarum takutik kēṟṟa irukkikaḷil muṟiyē iruntapiṉ, kuyiluva mākkaḷ neṟippaṭa niṟpa - iṭakki mutaliya kuyiluvak karuviyāḷar tām niṟka vēṇṭiya muṟippaṭi niṟka, valakkāl muṉ mitittu ēṟi araṅkattu - araṅkēṟum nāṭakak kaṇikiyākiya mātavi araṅkilē valakkāli muṉvittu ēṟi, valat tūṇ cērtal vaḻakku eṉap porunti - porumuka veḻiṉikku niliyiṭaṉāṉa valappakkat tūṇic cērtal muṟi yeṉṟu avviṭattic cērntu, inneṟi vakiyāl iṭat tūṇ cērnta tolneṟi iyaṟkit tōriya makaḷirum - mātavi vantēṟi niṉṟavāṟē orumuka veḻiṉikku niliyiṭaṉāṉa iṭappakkat tūṇiṉic cērntu niṉṟa paḻiya neṟiyiyaṟ kiyi yuṭiya tōriya maṭantiyarum tāṉum, cīriyal poliya nīr ala nīṅka - naṉmi mikavum tīmi nīṅkavum vēṇṭi, vāram iraṇṭum variciyiṟ pāṭa-ōroṟṟu vāram īroṟṟu vāram eṉṉum teyvap pāṭal iraṇṭiṉiyum muṟiyālē pāṭa, pāṭiya vārattu īṟṟil niṉṟu icikkum kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ ellām - pāṭiya teyvap pāṭaliṉ iṟutiyilē niṉṟu kūṭi iciyā niṟkum karuvi kaḷellām, kuḻal vaḻi niṉṟatu yāḻē - vaṅkiyattiṉ vaḻi yāḻp pāṭal niṉṟatāka, yāḻvaḻit taṇṇumi niṉṟatu takavē - yāḻp pāṭaliṉ vaḻiyē mattaḷam takavuṟa niṉṟatāka, taṇṇumip piṉvaḻi niṉṟatu muḻavē - mattaḷattiṉ vaḻiyē kuṭamuḻā niṉṟatāka, muḻavoṭu kūṭiniṉṟu icittatu āmantiriki - muḻavuṭaṉ kūṭiniṉṟu vācciyak kūṟukaḷi amittatu iṭakkiyiṉ ōciyāka, āmantirikiyōṭu antaram iṉṟi - iṭakkiyōṭu muṉ coṉṉa kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ aṉittum paruntum niḻalumpōla oṉṟāy niṟpa, koṭṭu iraṇṭu uṭiyatu ōr maṇṭilam āka - oru tāḷattiṟku iraṇṭu paṟṟāka, kaṭṭiya maṇṭilam patiṉoṉṟu pōkki - pañcatāḷap pirapantamākap kaṭṭappaṭṭa tēci yotti oru tāḷattiṟku iraṇṭu paṟṟākap pattum tīrvu oṉṟumākap patiṉoru paṟṟālē tēcik kūtti āṭi muṭittu, vanta muṟiyiṉ vaḻimuṟi vaḻāmal - ippaṭic ceyki nāṭaka nūlkaḷil aminta muṟiyākalāṉ ammuṟi vaḻuvāmal, antarak koṭṭu uṭaṉ aṭaṅkiya piṉṉar - antarakkoṭṭu eṉṟum mukam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum ivvottu āṭimuṭinta piṉṉar, mīttiṟam paṭāmi vakkāṇam pakuttup pāṟpaṭa niṉṟa pālip paṇmēl - maṅkalap paṇṇāy narappaṭiyavum uṭittā yirukkiṉṟa pālippaṇṇi aḷavu kōṭātapaṭi āḷattiyilē vittu ataṉmēlē, nāṉkiṉ orīiya naṉkaṉam aṟintu - maṅkalac colliṉi yuṭittāy nāluṟuppum kuṟipāṭillāta uruvukkuc coṟ paṭuttiyum icippaṭuttiyum aṟintu pāṭṭum koṭṭum kūttum nikaḻtti, mūṉṟu aḷantu oṉṟu koṭṭi ataṉi - mūṉṟottuṭiya maṭṭattilē eṭuttu ōrottuṭiya ēka tāḷattilē muṭittu, aitu maṇṭilattāl - aḻakiya maṇṭila niliyālē, kūṭi pōkki - tēcikku oṟṟit tottalum iraṭṭit tottalumē yākaliṉ tēcik kūṟellām āṭi muṭittu.
iṉi mārkkaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
āṟum nālum ammuṟi pōkki - pañcatāḷap pirapanta mākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa vaṭukilottiyum tēciyilottik kāṭṭiṉāṟ pōla iraṭṭik kiraṭṭiyāka āṭi, kūṟiya aintiṉ koḷki pōlap piṉṉiyum ammuṟi pēriya piṉṟi - muṉ collip pōnta tēciyippōla vaṭukum maṭṭattāḷam mutalāka ēkatāḷam antamāka vicāka niliyilē āṭi muṭitta piṉṉar, poṉ iyal pūṅkoṭi purintu uṭaṉ vakutteṉa - poṉṉāl iyaṉṟatōr pūṅkoṭiyāṉatu kūttu naṭittāṟ pōla, nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭippiṭittuk kāṭṭiṉaḷ ātaliṉ - tāṇṭavam niruttam nāṭṭiyam eṉṉum mūṉṟu kūṟu pāṭṭiṉum nāṭṭiyameṉṉum puṟa naṭatti nūlkaḷiṟ coṉṉa muṟimi tavaṟāmal avinayittup pāvakan tōṉṟa vilakkuṟuppup patiṉāṉkiṉ neṟivaḻuvāmal āṭikkāṭṭiṉaḷ ātalāl; 
129. 		aracaṉ mutaliyōr irunta piṉṉar eṉa viritturikka
134. 		tōriya makaḷirāvar āṭi mutirntavar,
“	inneṟi vakiyā liṭattūṇ cērvōḷ
toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ ṟōriya makaḷē”
eṉavum
“	talikkō larivi kuṇattoṭu porunti
nalattaku pāṭalu māṭalu mikkōḷ
iyaṟpaṭu kōtit tōriya makaḷē
eṉavum kūṟuvar. tōriya makaḷirum eṉṉum ummiyāl, tāṉum eṉpatu viritturikka. 
 135. 	cīriyal poliya eṉpataṟkut tāḷaviyalpu polivu peṟa eṉṟum, nīrala nīṅka eṉpataṟku avatāḷam nīṅka eṉṟum urittalumām.
 137 - 143	niṉṟatu niṉṟatu niṉṟatu niṉṟicittatu eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ āka eṉṉum coṟ kūṭṭi eccappaṭuttu, āmantiriki icittatāka, iṅṅaṉam vārattu īṟṟilē niṉṟu kūṭi yiciyāniṟkum kuyiluvak karuvikaḷellām antara miṉṟi oṉṟāy niṟpa eṉac coṉ muṭipu koḷka. āmantiriki - iṭakki yeṉṉum vācciyam. vārap pāṭal pāṭiya piṉ eṉṟamiyāl miṭaṟṟup pāṭalum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. yāḻ kuḻalvaḻi niṉṟāṟpōla miṭaṟṟuppāṭaliṉ vaḻiyum niṉṟa teṉka. 
147.		itaṉi antarakkoṭṭu eṉṟum, mukam eṉṟum, ottu eṉṟum peyar kūṟupa. inta ottu āṭiya piṉṉallatu nāṭaka makaḷ urukkāṭṭutal vaḻakkalla eṉpar. 
148 - 149	pālip paṇṇi mīttiṟam paṭāmi vakkāṇam vakuttu ataṉmēl eṉṟiyikka. vakkāṇam - āḷatti. 
150.		‘nāluṟuppuṅ kuṟipāṭillāta' eṉṉum iruvarurikkum 'nāṉkiṉ orīiya' eṉṉum mūlam poruttamātal iṉṟu; oruvā, oruvila eṉṟiṅṅaṉam pāṭamiruntirukkum pōlum. `nāluṟuppāvaṉa: ukkiram, turuvi, āpōkam, pirakali eṉpaṉa. īṇṭu nāluṟuppukkaḷum kuṟipāṭillāta uruveṉa vēṇṭiyatu, mūṉṟuṟuppālē varuvaṉavum uḷavātaliṉ. avi iraṇṭāmaṭiyē īṟṟaṭiyākap pāṭi muṭivaṉa. avi maṅkalattukkup poruntāveṉakkoḷka' eṉpar. 
152 - 154	ikkātiyiṉ 20-21 aṭikaḷiṉ poruḷ nōkkuka. mārkkam eṉiṉum vaṭuku eṉiṉum okkum. 
155.		aintu eṉpatu pañcatāḷam eṉṉum poruṭṭāka. kūṟiya eṉṉum aṭiyāl atu tēciyik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. 
158-159.	‘ikkātiyiṟ kūṟiya muttamiḻ vakiyum kāṭṭiṉaḷā taliṉ eṉṟumām' eṉpar tolli yuriyāciriyar iruvarum.
mātavi ciṟappup peṟṟatum kōvalaṉ avaḷi yuṟṟatum
	kāval vēntaṉ ...................................... maṟanteṉ.
159 - 175 	kāval vēntaṉ ilip pūṅkōti iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāmi -mātavi taṉ kūttukkum pāṭṭukkum aḻakukkum ēṟṟa muṟiyil vaḻuvāmal (kāṭṭiṉaḷātaliṉ) kāvali yuṭiya aracaṉatu pacci māliyuṭaṉ, talik kōl eyti - talikkōṟ peyar peṟṟu, tali araṅku ēṟi-ellā mutaṉmiyum peṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya muṉṉaraṅ kēṟap peṟṟu, vitimuṟi koḷkiyiṉ - innāṭakak kaṇikiyarkkut talivariciyeṉa nūlkaḷ vititta muṟippaṭi, āyirattu eṇkaḻañcu oru muṟiyākap peṟṟaṉaḷ - āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu poṉ oru muṟiyākap paricam peṟṟāḷ : atuvē - aṉṟu toṭaṅki atuvē nāṭōṟum paricamāka, `nūṟṟuppattu aṭukki eṭṭuk kaṭi niṟutta - nūṟṟip patiṉ maṭaṅkāka aṭukki ataṉ kaṭikkaṇṇē eṭṭi niṟuttiṉa (āyiratteṭṭu eṉṟa paṭi), vīṟu uyar pacumpoṉ peṟuvatu im māli - vīṟu peṟṟuyarnta pacumpoṉṉi viliyākap peṟuvatu im māli, immāli vāṅkunar cālum nam koṭikku eṉa - ivvaḷavu poṉ tantu im māliyi vāṅkic cūṭuvār mātavikku maṇamakaṉātal amiyum' eṉṟu colli, māṉ amar nōkki ōr kūṉi kikkoṭuttu - māṉ pōṉṟa nōkkiṉi yuṭiyavaḷākiya ōr kūṉikiyiṟ koṭuttu, nakara nampiyar tiritaru maṟukil - nakarattu āṇṭakic celvarkaḷ ulā varum perun teruvil, pakarvaṉar pōlvatōr pāṉmiyiṉ niṟutta - vilikku viṟpārip pōlvatōr paṇpiṉāl niṟutta, māmalar neṭuṅkaṇ mātavi māli kōvalaṉ vāṅki - ciṟanta tāmari malar pōṉṟa kaṇṇiyuṭiya mātaviyiṉ parica māliyik kōvalaṉ vāṅki, kūṉi taṉṉoṭum maṇamaṉipukku - kūṉiyuṭaṉē mātaviyiṉ maṇamaṉiyilē pukuntu, mātavi taṉṉōṭu aṇivuṟu vikaliṉ - avaḷuṭaṉ aṇinta aṉṟē, ayarntaṉaṉ mayaṅki - ayarntu mayaṅki, viṭutal aṟiyā viruppiṉaṉ āyiṉaṉ - nīṅka muṭiyāta viruppatti uṭiyaṉāyiṉāṉ ; vaṭu nīṅku ciṟappiṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya, taṉ maṉi akam maṟantu eṉ - taṉ maṉiviyiyum maṉiyiyum maṟantu eṉka.
160. 		ilip pūṅkōti - mātavi yeṉṟurippārumuḷar. 
 161. 		talikkōṟ peyar peṟutalāvatu talikkōli eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟutal ; teṉṉāṭṭup paḻiya kalveṭṭukkaḷil 1"tirunelvēli uṭiyār kōyil patiyilāril nakkaṉ urimi aḻakiya perumāḷāṉa urimi aḻakiya perumāḷ talikkōli" eṉavum, "patiyilāṉ nakkaṉ araṅkamāṉa jayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻat talikkōliyum, nakkaṉ pūmiyāṉa paramākkaviṭaṅkat talikkōliyum, nakkaṉ cōḻa viccātariyāṉa olōka mātēvi talikkōliyum, nakkaṉ pavaḻak kuṉṟāṉa maturāntakat talikkōliyum" eṉavum talikkōli eṉṉum paṭṭamum, ataṉippeṟṟa patiyilār palar peyarum varutal īṇṭu aṟiyattakkaṉa. 
162.		nāṭakak kaṇikiyar talivariciyāka āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu peṟutal nūl vaḻakkākum;
"	muṭṭil pāṇarum āṭiyaṉ makaḷirum
eṭṭoṭu puṇarnta āyiram poṉpeṟupa"
eṉpar.
 165. 	pacumpoṉ - kiḷicciṟi yeṉṉum poṉ. peṟuvatu - vili matikkap peṟuvatu. āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu poṉṉukku māli paṭikkaṭṭaḷi yāyiṟṟu.
166. 		koṭi eṉpatu mātavi yeṉṉum peyarukkup poruntiya tōr nayamuṭittu.
170.		māmalar - tirumakaḷ vāḻum malar eṉṟumām.
 173.	aṇivuṟu vikal - aṇinta appoḻutē eṉṟa paṭi. ayarttal - atuvēyātal. mayaṅkutal - aṟivu tirital.
174.		vaṭunīṅku ciṟappu - maṉiyāṭkuk kaṟpiṉ ciṟappum, maṉikkuc celvac ciṟappumām. vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ maṉiyakam maṟantu eṉṟatu nāṭakak kaṇikiyip paṟṟi avaḷ maṉiyiyē vāḻumiṭamākak koṇṭatu avaṉukku nīṅkāta vaṭuvā meṉak kuṟippiṉuṇarttiyavāṟām. maṟantu āyiṉaṉ eṉṟiyiyum. eṉ, aci.
mātaviyiṉ āṭaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟi maṉṉaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭi āṭalāciriyaṉ mutalāyiṉār oruṅkukūṭi, araṅkattu, vantaṉiceytu vaḻipaṭu talikkōli maṇṇiya piṉṉar ūrvalañ ceytu pukuntu muṉvikka, mātavi valakkāl muṉmitit tēṟi āṭik kāṭṭiṉaḷātaliṉ vēntaṉatu ilip pūṅkōtiyum talik kōṟpeyarum peṟṟaṉaḷ ; peṟṟapiṉ māliyik kūṉi kikkoṭuttu niṟutta, ataṉik kōvalaṉ, vāṅki, maṉipukku, ayarntu, mayaṅki, maṟantu viruppiṉaṉāyiṉaṉc eṉka.
iḵtu ellāvaṭiyum aḷavaṭiyāki muṭintamiyiṉ nilimaṇṭilavāciriyappā.

eṇṇum eḻuttum ............ vantu
pūmpukārp poṟṟoṭi mātavi - aḻakiya pukārnakariṟ piṟanta poṉvaḷi yaṇinta mātavi yeṉṉum kaṇiki, āṭu araṅkiṉ vantu - naṭikkum araṅkattilē vantu, eṇṇum eḻuttum - ellāk kalikaṭkum karuviyākiya kaṇitam ilakkaṇam eṉpa vaṟṟiyum, iyal aintum - iyaṟṟamiḻiṉ aintu pākupāṭṭiṉiyum, paṇ nāṉkum - icittamiḻiṉ nāṟperum paṇṇiyum, paṇniṉṟa kūttup patiṉoṉṟum - nāṭakattamiḻiṉ iṉimiyuṭiya patiṉoru kūttiṉiyum, taṉ vākkiṉāl-taṉvākkiṉālum kūttiṉālum, maṇṇiṉmēl pōkkiṉāḷ-puvimuḻutum aṟintu pukaḻumpaṭi ceytāḷ.
kūttiṉālum eṉa viritturittukkoḷka.
araṅkēṟṟu kāti muṟṟiṟṟu. 
4. antimālic ciṟappuccey kāti 
(kaṭalāṭi nilamaṭantiyāṉavaḷ 'ulaka muḻutāṇṭa oru taṉittikiriyi uṭiya uravōṉik kāṇkiṉṟilēṉ' eṉṟu ticimukam pacantu, taṉ kaṇavaṉākiya paritiyik keṭuttu varuntuṅ kāli, muṭiyuṭip pēraracar nīṅkiya aṟṟam pārttuk kuṭikaḷ varuntumāṟu vantu pukunta kuṟunila maṉṉarpōla mālip poḻutu vantatu. piṉṉar, iḷiyarāyiṉum pakiyaracukaṭiyum pāṇṭiyar kula mutalākiya veṇpiṟi tōṉṟi, māliyākiya kuṟumpiṉiyōṭṭi mīṉaraciṉi āḷāniṉṟatu. appoḻutu mātaviyāṉavaḷ nilāmuṟṟattilē malaramaḷiyil ūṭalum kūṭalum kōvalaṟ kaḷittu iṉpuṟukiṉṟāḷ; avaḷippōṉṟē kaṇavarōṭu kūṭiya makaḷir palarum pūñcēkkiyil āvipōlum koḻunarmārpil oṭuṅkik kāvimalarpōlum kaṇṇāṟ kaḷittuyil eytukiṉṟaṉar. kaṇṇakiyō kātalaṉip pirintamiyāl maṅkalavaṇiyaṉṟip piṟitaṇi aṇiyāmalum, vēṟu oppaṉi yoṉṟumiṉṟiyum, nutal tilakam iḻakkavum, kaṇ añcaṉam maṟakkavum, kūntal neyyaṇi tuṟakkavum kiyaṟṟa neñcuṭaṉ kalaṅkukiṉṟaṉaḷ. avaḷ pōṉṟē kātalarp pirinta mātar palarum ūtulik kurukupōl veytuyirttuk kaṇkaḷ muttiṉi utirkka varuntukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ivvāṟāka, iravu nīṅkum vikaṟikāṟum makarakkoṭiyiyuṭiya maṉmataṉ naṭuyāmattum tuyilātu tiritalāl nakarkāval naṉiciṟappatāyiṟṟu)
		virikatir parappi ulakamuḻu tāṇṭa
orutaṉit tikiri uravōṟ kāṇēṉ
aṅkaṇ vāṉat taṇinilā virikkun
tiṅkaḷañ celvaṉ yāṇṭuḷaṉ kolleṉat
5		ticimukam pacantu cemmalark kaṇkaḷ
muḻunīr vāra muḻumeyum paṉittut
tirinī rāṭi yirunila maṭanti
aricukeṭuttalamvarum allaṟ kālik
kaṟikeḻu kuṭikaḷ kitali vippa
10		aṟipōku kuṭikaḷo ṭorutiṟam paṟṟi
valampaṭu tāṉi maṉṉa rilvaḻip
pulampaṭa iṟutta viruntiṉ maṉṉariṉ
tāḻtuṇi tuṟantōr taṉittuya reytak
kātalarp puṇarntōr kaḷimakiḻ veytak
15		kuḻalvaḷar mulliyiṟ kōvalar tammoṭu
maḻalit tumpi vāyvit tūta
aṟukāṟ kuṟumpeṟin tarumpupoti vācañ
ciṟukāṟ celvaṉ maṟukil tūṟṟa
elvaḷi makaḷir maṇiviḷak keṭuppa
20		mallal mūtūr mālivan tiṟutteṉa
iḷiya rāyiṉum pakiyaracu kaṭiyuñ
cerumāṇ teṉṉar kulamuta lākaliṉ
antivā ṉattu veṇpiṟi tōṉṟip
puṉkaṇ mālik kuṟumpeṟin tōṭṭip
25		pāṉmiyil tiriyātu pāṟkatir parappi
mīṉara cāṇṭa veḷḷi viḷakkattu
ilvaḷar mulliyoṭu malliki yaviḻnta
palpūñ cēkkip paḷḷiyuṭ polintu
centukirk kōvi ceṉṟēn talkul
30		antukiṉ mēkali yacintaṉa varunta
nilavuppayaṉ koḷḷum neṭunilā muṟṟattuk
kalaviyum pulaviyuṅ kātalaṟ kaḷittāṅku
ārva neñcamoṭu kōvalaṟ ketirik
kōlaṅ koṇṭa mātavi yaṉṟiyum
35		kuṭatici maruṅkiṉ veḷḷayir taṉṉoṭu
kuṇatici maruṅkiṟ kārakil tuṟantu
vaṭamalip piṟanta vāṉkēḻ vaṭṭattut
teṉmalip piṟanta cantaṉa maṟukat
tāmarik koḻumuṟit tātupaṭu ceḻumalark
40		kāmaru kuvaḷik kaḻunīr māmalarp	
pintaḷirp paṭali parūukkā ḻāram
cuntarac cuṇṇat tukaḷoṭum aḷiic
cintupu parinta ceḻumpūñ cēkki
mantamā rutattu mayaṅkiṉar malintāṅku
45		āviyaṅ koḻuna rakalat toṭuṅkik
kāviyaṅ kaṇṇār kaḷittuyi leyta
añceñ cīṟaṭi yaṇicilam poḻiya
meṉtukil alkul mēkali nīṅkak
koṅki muṉṟiṟ kuṅkuma meḻutāḷ
50		maṅkala vaṇiyiṟ piṟitaṇi makiḻāḷ
koṭuṅkuḻi tuṟantu vaṭintuvīḻ kātiṉaḷ
tiṅkaḷ vāṇmukam ciṟuviyar piriyac
ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ añcaṉam maṟappap
pavaḷa vāṇutal tilakam iḻappat
55		tavaḷa vāṇaki kōvalaṉ iḻappa
miyiruṅ kūntal neyyaṇi maṟappak
kiyaṟu neñcattuk kaṇṇaki yaṉṟiyum
kātalarp pirinta mātar nōtaka
ūtulik kurukiṉ uyirttaṉa roṭuṅki
60		vēṉiṟ paḷḷi mēvātu kaḻintu
kūtirp paḷḷik kuṟuṅkaṇ aṭittu
maliyattu āramu maṇimut tāramum
alarmuli yākattu aṭiyātu varuntat
tāḻik kuvaḷiyoṭu taṇceṅ kaḻunīr
65		vīḻpūñ cēkki mēvātu kaḻiyat
tuṇipuṇa raṉṉat tūviyiṟ ceṟitta
iṇiyaṇi mēmpaṭat tiruntutuyil peṟāatu
uṭipperuṅ koḻunarō ṭūṭaṟ kālat
tiṭikkumi ḻeṟintu kaṭikkuḻi yōṭṭik
70		kalaṅkā vuḷḷaṅ kalaṅkak kaṭicivantu
vilaṅkinimir neṭuṅkaṇ pulampumut tuṟippa
aṉṉa meṉṉaṭi naṉṉīrp poyki
āmpal nāṟun tēmpoti naṟuvirit
tāmaric cevvāyt taṇṇaṟaṟ kūntal
75		pāṇvāy vaṇṭu nōtiṟam pāṭak
kāṇvaru kuvaḷik kaṇmalar viḻippap
puḷvāy muracamoṭu poṟimayir vāraṇattu
muḷvāyc caṅkam muṟimuṟi yārppa
uravunīrp parappiṉ ūrtuyi leṭuppi
80		iravuttalip peyarum vikaṟi kāṟum
ariyiruḷ yāmattum pakalum tuñcāṉ
virimalar vāḷiyoṭu karuppuvil lēnti
makara velkoṭi mintaṉ tiritara
nakaraṅ kāval naṉiciṟan tatuveṉ.
